- **Arc** ([Release v22.118.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.118.0)): `IOccurrenceCommandExecutor` refuses an immediate (non-transactional) append inside a keyed occurrence command before it is sent; the attempt fails with nothing appended and no effect receipt. Use the unit of work (`.Transactional`) for appends in occurrence commands. See [Executing commands for occurrences](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/blob/main/Documentation/backend/csharp/chronicle/commands/occurrence-commands.md). (#3354)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.118.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.118.0)): Updated Cratis.Chronicle to 19.81.0. (#3354)
- **cli** ([Release v3.78.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.78.0)): Bundles Stage 4.146.0 and Screenplay 4.212.2; `cratis run` starts `cratis/stage:4.146.0` by default (#395)
- **cli** ([Release v3.78.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.78.0)): A model `cratis render` refuses now lists every independent refusal at once, so fixing it no longer reveals new refusals one render at a time (#395)
- **cli** ([Release v3.78.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.78.0)): `cratis run` on the semantic engine refuses only the commands and queries that reach a reducer or code-bodied validation, with `STAGE-IMPL-003`, and runs the rest of the model (#395)
- **cli** ([Release v3.78.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.78.0)): Rendered applications pin Arc 22.112.1 and Chronicle 19.78.0 (#395)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.147.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.147.0)): The specification executor and the semantic Host no longer refuse a whole model because a command has a described handler (ESM v23). A specification or command that reaches the handler body is reported as Unsupported with the new `ImplementationBody` capability, after authorization and validation have run, so denied and invalid outcomes for that command, and every specification that does not reach the handler, still run. The Cratis renderer still refuses described handlers with `STAGE-IMPL-002`. (part of #316)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.147.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.147.0)): Specification run reports can now carry the `ImplementationBody` capability. A reader built against an earlier Stage version rejects that value.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.146.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.146.0)): The Cratis renderer runs every independent admission check — version features, implementation envelopes, typed contexts, event lineage, slice and specification admission — and lists all their refusals together, so fixing a model no longer reveals new refusals one render at a time. A model refused by the target profile or by an unaudited ESM version still stops at that refusal first. A refusal reported by two checks is listed once. Refusals collected before an unexpected model shape is reached are no longer lost. Plans that render are unchanged. (part of #500)
- **AI** ([Release v7.74.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.74.0)): A decision call no longer appends `DecisionUsageRecorded`, and a provider usage refresh no longer appends `AIProviderUsageSnapshotRecorded`. Decision usage is reported on the `Cratis.AI.Decisions` meter: the counters `cratis.ai.decision.input_tokens`, `cratis.ai.decision.output_tokens`, `cratis.ai.decision.calls`, `cratis.ai.decision.decisions` and `cratis.ai.decision.choices`, tagged `decision.engine`, `model` and `topic`. Provider usage is reported on the `Cratis.AI.Providers.UsageReporting` meter as the gauge `cratis.ai.provider.consumed_tokens`, tagged `provider` and `availability`. Add both meters to your OpenTelemetry setup to collect them. (#651)
- **AI** ([Release v7.74.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.74.0)): `AllAIProviderUsageSnapshots` keeps its name and result shape. It is now served from the in-process usage cache and only returns the providers configured in the current namespace. After a restart it is empty until each provider's usage is refreshed again.
- **AI** ([Release v7.74.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.74.0)): `DecisionUsageForLastThirtyDays` no longer receives new data, because nothing appends `DecisionUsageRecorded` any more.
- **AI** ([Release v7.74.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.74.0)): The constructors of `ProviderUsageLevels` and `DecisionUsageRecorder` no longer take `ICommandPipeline`, and `IRecentUsageSnapshots` has a new `Observe()` member. This only matters if you construct these types or implement the interface yourself.
- **AI** ([Release v7.74.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.74.0)): Cratis.AI now depends on Cratis.Fundamentals 7.25.1 (was 7.19.4).
- **Stage** ([Release v4.145.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.145.0)): The specification executor refuses only the specifications that reach a reducer or a validation implementation body, and the semantic Host refuses only the commands and queries that reach one. Previously a single such body anywhere refused every specification and every command. A body is reached through the command under test, commands invoked by reactions, concept rules on command properties and query arguments, and reducers fed by given, appended or produced events, including events from an action expected to be refused. (part of #513)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.145.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.145.0)): These refusals carry the new diagnostic code `STAGE-IMPL-003`, naming the body the specification or command reaches. (part of #316)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.117.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.117.0)): Updated Cratis.Chronicle to 19.77.2 and Cratis.Metrics.Roslyn to 7.25.0. (part of #3288)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.80.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.80.0)): Cratis.Orleans is updated to 1.23.0.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.80.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.80.0)): A step that times out is run again, up to five attempts, before its partition is reported as failed, so a busy observer no longer turns into a cascade of failed partitions. (part of #4991)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.80.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.80.0)): A failed job step records one short message instead of a multi-kilobyte stack trace. (part of #4644)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.80.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.80.0)): Replay keeps at most 16 partition steps active at once instead of starting all of them. (part of #4991)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.80.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.80.0)): A job recorded as Running whose steps all concluded more than two minutes ago is no longer treated as running, so `observers replay` starts a real job and a replaying observer without a live job is routed again. (part of #4991)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.80.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.80.0)): The replay-complete notification to the observer is retried instead of being fired once and forgotten. (part of #4991)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.80.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.80.0)): Observers coalesce handled-event counts and failure follow-up writes, which takes load off the observer grain. (part of #4991)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.80.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.80.0)): The kernel statistics projections are no longer fully replayed on every restart, and superseded read model generations are deleted after a replay. (part of #4692) (part of #4644)
- **Scene** ([Release v4.69.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.69.0)): The `Drawer` menu mode now opens an off-canvas menu over a dimming scrim, and `Overlay` no longer dims; the hover-driven rail is `Reveal` alone. A touch width now always resolves to Drawer (#196)
- **cli** ([Release v3.77.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.77.0)): Bundles Stage 4.143.0 and Screenplay 4.211.2, so the rendered application scenarios require the custom two-column manual command form (part of Cratis/Screenplay#721)
- **cli** ([Release v3.77.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.77.0)): Rendering refuses an unknown runtime package or a missing layout through Stage's own profile input resolution, so Studio and the CLI refuse identically (part of Cratis/Screenplay#722)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.144.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.144.0)): Generated applications pin Arc 22.112.1 for both the .NET and the frontend packages, Chronicle 19.78.0 with the `cratis/chronicle:19.78.0-development` kernel image, and on the frontend Fundamentals 7.25.1 and Components 4.29.0. Existing applications see the new versions on their next regeneration. (#493)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.144.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.144.0)): Stage itself builds and ships with Arc 22.112.1, Chronicle 19.78.0 and Fundamentals 7.25.1, and its bundled kernel image is `cratis/chronicle:19.78.0-development`. (#493)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.23.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.23.0)): `IJobsManager.Stop` now returns `Result<StopJobError>` instead of `Task`; update callers that await it as a plain task if they need the outcome.
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.23.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.23.0)): A running job is watched: it counts its steps in storage and finalizes itself when every step is done, counting `Failed` and `CompletedWithFailure` as failed, instead of staying `Running` when a step's report never arrived.
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.23.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.23.0)): A step persists its terminal status first and then delivers it to the job, retrying with backoff until acknowledged. A delivery that never succeeds no longer marks the step failed; the job accepts each outcome once per step and attempt.
- **Arc** ([Release v22.116.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.116.0)): Subscribing to a query without `[AllowAnonymous]` or a guest policy over the hub's WebSocket or SSE transport now applies the host's default authorization before the query runs, as a direct request to that query does. A caller the host would turn away receives an Unauthorized frame, and SSE subscribe returns 401. `[AllowAnonymous]` and guest-policy queries stay reachable anonymously. (#2952)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.116.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.116.0)): Non-observable queries performed over the hub now pass the registered emission guards before their result is sent. A guard that denies sends Unauthorized; a guard that suppresses sends nothing. (#2955)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.115.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.115.0)): Validation results now always report their members as camelCase field paths (`name`, `address.street`, `items[0].name`), the same form command and concept validators already used. Server-side assertions against `ValidationResult.Members` must use the camelCase field path instead of `nameof(Property)`.
- **AI** ([Release v7.73.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.73.0)): The model-authoring skill teaches that one proposal may remove and add unrelated references together, that only a reference with the same text both removed and added is refused, and how to read the located conflict's document, path and occurrences instead of splitting the batch. Use `propose-move` or a semantic migration to keep a moved reference's identity. (#762)
- **AI** ([Release v7.73.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.73.0)): The Screenplay skills describe a file or inline command handler as refused with `PLAY0800`, whose required `subject` argument names the command: a permanent limitation that is gap-filled in code, not something a later ESM version adds. `PLAY0268` keeps meaning "not representable in a supported ESM version yet". (#761)
- **AI** ([Release v7.73.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.73.0)): The pinned Screenplay compiler, standalone tool and contract move to 4.211.1.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.142.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.142.0)): The semantic Host decides a command on the session world, then reads the facts other writers appended since and commits the decision against them. Only an outside fact the decision depends on turns the command into a concurrency conflict; unrelated outside facts are absorbed and the session continues. Refusals keep their outcome. A batch whose write loses the race is retried with the same decision only when its reactions invoked no commands. (#474)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.142.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.142.0)): Stage now builds against Screenplay 4.206.0.
- **Arc** ([Release v22.114.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.114.0)): Protected-read refusals PLAY0781–PLAY0785 are attributed to the affected command and read through Screenplay's typed diagnostic arguments, and each refused read is reported separately. (#3344)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.114.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.114.0)): Updated Cratis.Screenplay to 4.208.0. (#3344)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.141.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.141.0)): Applications Stage renders and scaffolds now target Arc 22.112.0.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.141.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.141.0)): The renderer still refuses, with `STAGE-ESM-046` naming the property, a compared DateTime concept that carries compliance or is optional, and a compared DateTime property whose name is not camel case.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.141.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.141.0)): For a command with an authorization policy, an unauthorized caller sending an unbindable date body gets a validation error rather than 403, because Arc binds the body before evaluating Stage's named policies (Cratis/Arc#3341).
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.211.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.211.2)): The canonical screen-composition corpus renders a manual two-column command form (1fr and 2fr columns, a 24px gap and a field spanning both columns) so live Stage and generated React are held to the same grid geometry. (#721)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.211.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.211.0)): `propose-source`, `propose-ast` and the other authoring tools accept a batch that removes one reference and adds an unrelated one, the same as proposing the two edits one after the other. A batch is refused only when a reference with the same text is both removed and added, because its identity cannot be told apart from a move; use `propose-move` or semantic migrations for a real move. See [reference continuity](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/blob/main/Documentation/screenplay/mcp/authoring-tools.md). (#783)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.211.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.211.0)): MCP responses write document identities and document paths as plain strings: `conflicts[].document`, `conflicts[].path` and `conflicts[].otherPath`, and the document fields in a `propose-move` report. A conflict without a document reports `null`. (#783)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.210.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.210.0)): A file or inline command handler is now refused with its own error code `PLAY0800` (command handler requires a constrained implementation attachment) instead of `PLAY0268`, with a required `subject` argument naming the command. `PLAY0268` keeps meaning a construct is not representable in a supported executable model version yet, so tools can tell a permanent limitation from one that a later version admits. Tools that matched the handler refusal by `PLAY0268` and its message text switch to `PLAY0800`. See the [diagnostics reference](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/blob/main/Documentation/screenplay/diagnostics.md). (#852)
- **AI** ([Release v7.71.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.71.0)): The Screenplay command-surface skill teaches that, from Screenplay 4.209.0, a protected read whose key (or any part of a composite key) is a shape the event store refuses rejects as missing state, even when an instance is stored at that key. The refused shapes are empty, whitespace-only, exactly `*`, containing `#`, and leading or trailing whitespace. Only protected reads change, and the skill shows how to keep such keys out of read keys. (#756)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.140.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.140.0)): Bundles Scene 4.68.2 (engine and model NuGet, `@cratis/scene.*` and the default blueprint on npm), which carries the template layout and contrast fixes this vector found; the frozen render digests that embed the scene pin are refreshed.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.140.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.140.0)): A new `Verification/blueprint-templates` vector: the default blueprint's MasterDetail, Dashboard and ProfileSettings screen templates are applied to screens in `model/templates.play` and rendered (a) on the live Stage image (nothing approved, the blueprint ships in the Stage) and (b) in a generated React application built the way its author would (Debug backend with generated Arc proxies, npm install, frontend build). The browser scenario asserts, per template, that every slot is filled, the page does not overflow, the grid layout holds and axe finds no WCAG A/AA violation. Both run in CI (`live` job step and a new `generated-react-blueprint-templates` job).
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.209.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.209.0)): A protected command read whose key, or any part of a composite key, is a shape the event store refuses (empty, whitespace-only, exactly `*`, containing `#`, or with leading or trailing whitespace) now rejects with the same `Validation` outcome and message as a missing view, even when the world holds an instance at that key. Other uses of such a key, including command identifiers, are unchanged. The canonical protected-reads corpus gains vectors for these shapes so hosts can prove they map their store's refusal to this outcome. See [decision 0091](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/blob/main/decisions/0091-protected-reads-treat-keys-the-event-store-refuses-as-missing.md) and [Keys the event store refuses](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/blob/main/Documentation/screenplay/commands.md). (#843)
- **AI** ([Release v7.70.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.70.0)): The Screenplay and Stage skills teach that rendering admits concepts with `generate rule` and emits the attached C# body as the concept's generation factory, with `STAGE-ESM-019`, `STAGE-ESM-020` and `STAGE-ESM-022` for bodies it refuses, while the Host and the legacy syntax engine still refuse commands that generate such a value with `STAGE-ESM-028`. (#752)
- **AI** ([Release v7.70.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.70.0)): The skills teach ESM v27 composite read-model keys downstream: the semantic Host runs them, the specification executor refuses specifications over them, and rendering refuses them with `STAGE-ESM-047` and `STAGE-ESM-048`. `cratis render` from CLI 3.75.0 admits ESM v1–v27 and blocks v28 with `CLI-RENDER-004`. (#752)
- **AI** ([Release v7.70.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.70.0)): The skills teach that Stage 4.138.0 renders protected command reads as Arc read protection, which reads it still refuses with `STAGE-ESM-044`, and that `cratis render` gets this from the first CLI that bundles Stage 4.138.0. (#752)
- **AI** ([Release v7.70.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.70.0)): The skills describe `cratis run`'s semantic engine as the default (CLI 3.73.0; `--engine` since 3.64.0) and the EventModel engine as legacy, no longer recommending it as a fallback. (#752)
- **AI** ([Release v7.70.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.70.0)): The toolchain and event-modeling skills state that the language is frozen at ESM v1–v28 and link [the freeze decision](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/blob/main/decisions/0092-the-language-is-frozen-at-esm-v28.md): model with what v1–v28 offers and report a typed refusal downstream as a platform gap. (#752)
- **AI** ([Release v7.70.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.70.0)): The protected-reads reference lists the typed arguments of PLAY0781–PLAY0785 and the published `reason` keywords, so tools read the arguments instead of parsing message text. (#751)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.113.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.113.0)): A read-protection conflict now says which event changed the read model when Chronicle reports it, for example "BookStock 'isbn-1' changed after BorrowBook read it (BookBorrowed at 1042)", and the validation result's state carries the event type and sequence number (#3242)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.113.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.113.0)): Arc uses Chronicle's public `SuspendReadRegions()` for nested commands instead of reflection, and requires Chronicle 19.76.0 (#3242)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.139.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.139.0)): Stage now audits ESM v22 models (`reaction batches atomic`) instead of refusing them as an unknown version with `STAGE-ESM-016`. Rendering and the Host refuse atomic reaction batches with `STAGE-ESM-043`, and the rest of the model is checked as for v23 and later models. Running and rendering atomic batches follows in a later release. (part of #422)
- **cli** ([Release v3.76.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.76.0)): Bundles Stage 4.138.0: `cratis render` uses its renderer and `cratis run` starts `cratis/stage:4.138.0` by default. (#388)
- **cli** ([Release v3.76.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.76.0)): `cratis render` renders ESM v25 protected command reads (`command reads protected`) as Arc read protection: each command with `reads` gets `[ReadProtection(On)]` and a read-model `Handle` parameter per read. Reads Stage cannot render faithfully, such as reads combined with constrained events or read paths in `produces when`, are still refused with `STAGE-ESM-044`, which now names the reason. See [cratis render](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/blob/main/Documentation/reference/screenplay.md). (#388)
- **cli** ([Release v3.76.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.76.0)): Generated single-instance queries keyed by a text or UUID value now take an event source id; UUID keys are matched as text, so callers send the canonical lowercase form. (#388)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.21.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.21.0)): Admission is asked before `Start`, before every `Resume` (explicit, `IJobsManager.Resume`, rehydration after a silo dies, and recovery), and before every stage after the first. A refused job that is running is stopped and drained, and it is not resumed while the owner keeps refusing. A refused stage stops the job. (#81)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.21.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.21.0)): An exception or timeout in `MayRun` fails the start or resume and changes nothing. It is never a refusal. A stage whose admission cannot be answered is asked again on a timer. (#81)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.21.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.21.0)): `MayRun` runs inside the job's turn, so it must not call the job, its steps, or a non-interleaving method of a grain that may be waiting on the job's start or resume. To change what running jobs may do, call `StopAndDrain` first, then refuse them from `MayRun`. Silos older than 1.21 do not ask, so the guarantee holds once every silo runs 1.21 or later. (#81)
- **cli** ([Release v3.75.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.75.0)): Bundles Stage 4.137.0: `cratis run` starts `cratis/stage:4.137.0` by default, and its default `semantic` engine runs ESM v27 models with composite read-model keys. The `eventmodel` engine refuses them. (#386)
- **cli** ([Release v3.75.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.75.0)): The CLI's Screenplay compiler (`cratis screenplay`, `cratis render` and the Screenplay MCP server) is now Screenplay 4.204.0, which reads ESM v27. (#386)
- **cli** ([Release v3.75.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.75.0)): `cratis render` admits ESM v1 through v27. Stage 4.137.0 does not render composite read-model keys yet: read models and projections keyed by several parts report `STAGE-ESM-048`, single-instance queries by key parts report `STAGE-ESM-047`, and nothing is published. ESM v28 and later are blocked by `CLI-RENDER-004`. See [cratis render](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/blob/main/Documentation/reference/screenplay.md). (#386)
- **cli** ([Release v3.75.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.75.0)): Generated applications now reference Arc 22.109.0 and Chronicle 19.71.0. (#386)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.112.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.112.1)): `useQuery`: changing an optional argument now fetches again, as changing a required argument already did. (#3312)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.77.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.77.3)): Repeated strongly consistent reads of the same key no longer reuse earlier work. Each read costs roughly what a first read costs, and that cost grows with the number of events for the key. Prefer a materialized projection for long histories that are read often, or that a reactor reads on every event. (part of #4844)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.112.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.112.0)): Generated default output for applications whose commands use read protection `On` is now an ESM v25 document. Reads under `Off`, `Measure` or `[Unprotected]` stay advisory and are not written as `reads` in a document that declares protected reads. When `MaximumExecutableModelVersion` is below v25, the output is unchanged and a warning names the version needed.
- **Arc** ([Release v22.112.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.112.0)): In authoring output, a read is named after its view when the parameter name matches it (`reads Current by sourceId`), and `[LookUpBy]` is honored in every read protection mode.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.138.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.138.0)): Generated single-instance queries keyed by a plain text or UUID value now take an event source id, which fixes Arc analyzer ARC0015 in generated applications. UUID keys are matched as text, so callers must send the canonical lowercase form. (part of #440)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.138.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.138.0)): Protected reads that cannot be rendered faithfully, such as reads combined with constrained events or read paths in `produces when`, are still refused with STAGE-ESM-044, now naming the specific reason. (part of #440)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.77.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.77.2)): The Kernel runs on Cratis.Orleans 1.20.0. Stopping or deactivating a catch-up or replay job step now drains it within the job step stop timeout instead of waiting for the grain deactivation timeout; a step stopped mid-checkpoint resumes from its last durable checkpoint. (#5004)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.137.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.137.0)): Stage now uses Screenplay 4.204.0 and admits ESM v27 models. The semantic Host runs read models with composite keys end to end: projections keyed by named parts, removals, list queries that filter on any part, modeled queries by parts, and the read-model browser, which shows each key as an object of its parts. Composite-keyed read models get no conventional `Get<ReadModel>ById` query. (part of #481)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.137.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.137.0)): The specification executor refuses specifications over composite-keyed read models as unsupported (projection), naming the composite key. (part of #481)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.137.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.137.0)): Rendering refuses composite read-model keys with typed diagnostics instead of failing: `STAGE-ESM-048` for a read model with several key parts or a projection keyed by parts, and `STAGE-ESM-047` for a single-instance query by parts until Chronicle can look up composite keys (https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/issues/4265). The legacy syntax engine refuses composite-keyed read models with `STAGE-ESM-048`. (part of #481)
- **AI** ([Release v7.69.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.69.0)): The render skills say `cratis render` accepts models up to ESM v26 from Cratis CLI 3.74.0, with Stage refusing the constructs it cannot render yet through typed `STAGE-ESM-*` diagnostics, and explain what a CLI reports for a newer model: `CLI-RENDER-004`, or the binder's `PLAY0268` when its bundled compiler predates the construct.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.77.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.77.0)): Testing scenarios now register precise event and read-model schemas, because the in-process kernel advertises current capabilities by default. (part of #4911)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.77.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.77.0)): SQL stores get an additive event-store migration, `ES-Occurrences-v19_76_0`, for lifecyc
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.20.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.20.0)): A job whose silo dies during a drain finishes the drain when it next activates and is never resumed; `Resume` is refused while a drain is pending. (part of #81)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.20.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.20.0)): A step's stop report from before a `Resume` no longer counts against the resumed job, and a step stopped just before the resume is started again in the resumed execution. Silos older than 1.20 keep the earlier behavior. (part of #81)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.20.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.20.0)): `OnStepCompletedOrStopped` can run again for a step after a crash, or not at all, so it must be idempotent and owners should not take results from it alone. Use `StopAndDrain` only once every silo runs 1.20 or later. (part of #81)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.136.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.136.0)): Rendering now admits concepts that use `generate rule` (ESM v21). Stage compile-checks the attached C# body against the BCL and Fundamentals and emits it as the concept's `IGeneratableInContext<T>.New(GenerationContext)` factory, so the rendered application generates the value with the tenant and time of the operation. A body that does not compile or that produces a compiler warning is refused with `STAGE-ESM-019` at its source location; type or namespace shadowing is refused with `STAGE-ESM-022`, and a missing attachment with `STAGE-ESM-020`. The Host and the legacy syntax engine still refuse commands that generate a `generate rule` value with `STAGE-ESM-028`. (#414)
- **cli** ([Release v3.74.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.74.0)): `cratis render` admits ESM v21 through v26. Stage plans each model and refuses the constructs it cannot render yet with typed `STAGE-ESM-*` diagnostics, so nothing is published for them; for example, models compiled to ESM v22 report `STAGE-ESM-016` with Stage 4.135.0. ESM v27 and later are still blocked by `CLI-RENDER-004`. See [cratis render](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/blob/main/Documentation/reference/screenplay.md). (#384)
- **cli** ([Release v3.74.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.74.0)): Bundles Stage 4.135.0: `cratis render` uses its renderer and `cratis run` starts `cratis/stage:4.135.0` by default. (#384)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.111.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.111.0)): The generator requires Cratis.Screenplay 4.203.0.
- **Arc** ([Release v22.111.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.111.0)): A concept `Matches` rule that uses regex lookaround is withheld and reported, because Screenplay cannot express it.
- **Arc** ([Release v22.110.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.110.0)): A command body that cannot be read is reported only after the command's authorization has run, on both the execute and the `/validate` endpoint. An unauthorized caller now gets `403` instead of `400`. Policies with a requirement that reads the command cannot run without it, so for those commands an unbindable body still gives a validation error. (#3341)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.110.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.110.0)): A body that cannot be read now gives a `malformedRequest` validation result naming the failing member (for example `from` or `items[2].when`). The message stays generic, so no parser or type details are exposed. (#3341)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.110.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.110.0)): Controller-based commands report model binding errors with plain member names (`from`) instead of JSON paths (`$.from`). (#3341)
- **AI** ([Release v7.67.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.67.0)): The Stage and Screenplay skills say Stage admits ESM v26: its Host and specification executor run Date and DateTime comparisons in command conditions and validation rules, while rendering them is refused with `STAGE-ESM-046`, so delivery goes through gap-fill.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.135.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.135.1)): Applications Stage renders and scaffolds now target Arc 22.109.0 and Chronicle 19.71.0, and the Stage Host image runs the Chronicle 19.71.0 kernel. This prepares rendering of protected command reads with Arc read protection. (part of #440)
- **cli** ([Release v3.73.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.73.0)): `cratis run` runs models on Stage's semantic engine when `--engine` is omitted, for files and folders. To keep the previous behavior, run with `--engine eventmodel`; it stays available as an explicit fallback. See [Runtime engines](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/blob/main/Documentation/reference/run.md#runtime-engines). (#324)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.19.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.19.0)): Deactivating a job step cancels its work and waits for it within Orleans' deactivation timeout. The step stays recoverable: it writes no final status and reports nothing to the job, and recovery runs it again. (#74)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.19.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.19.0)): A job step reports exactly one outcome per run, after `Stop` has returned. A stop from the job no longer waits for the step's report, so stopping a job no longer loops back through the step.
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.19.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.19.0)): A step's state snapshot is copied with the Orleans `DeepCopier<TState>`. Silos register it through the serializer; a specification host such as Orleans.TestKit must register it, otherwise the step fails with a message naming the missing registration.
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.19.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.19.0)): Steps that still call their own grain reference from their work make deactivation wait for that work, up to Orleans' deactivation timeout. Move those calls to `TryInvokeOnActivation`.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.135.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.135.0)): Rendering a model that compares `Date` or `DateTime` values in commands is refused with STAGE-ESM-046, on the renderer and the default sandbox engine, until generated applications can compare them exactly. (part of #473)
- **cli** ([Release v3.72.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.72.0)): Bundles Stage 4.134.0 (a navigation item authored with `slot side` renders into the slot the layout declares, Cratis/Stage#467, Cratis/Stage#456) and Screenplay 4.201.0.
- **cli** ([Release v3.72.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.72.0)): The `Integration/Cli/Shell` scenario clicks the `Knowledge base` item (`slot side`) of the Support sidebar and asserts the page stays in the primary outlet, the target renders in the side pane, both items are active, and `Close` leaves only the page active - on a live `cratis run` Stage (own root and blueprint root) and on the application `cratis render` generated.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.204.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.204.0)): Joins, children, `remove via join`, `every`, `all`, `nested`, `clear with`, `variant` and reducers on a composite-keyed read model, and list queries with a `by` block over one, are refused with PLAY0268 instead of the whole read model being refused. (#599)
- **cli** ([Release v3.71.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.71.0)): Bundles Stage 4.132.0 (and the `cratis/stage:4.132.0` image `cratis run` starts), which renders the specifications, one-row summary projections and live collections of the CRM, Ticketing, Approvals, Inventory, HR, Project tracker, Administration and Orders samples without simplifying them (Cratis/Stage#478).
- **Stage** ([Release v4.134.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.134.0)): The Stage draws a navigation item that names a slot the shell declares (`slot side`) in a closable pane beside the page, through Scene 4.67.0's `ApplicationHost` (Cratis/Scene#184): both items are active, `Close` empties the pane, the pane survives navigation of the page and is restored from a reload or a deep link; a shell without the slot opens the target in the primary outlet.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.134.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.134.0)): A new `uiprofile-root` conformance vector: the canonical corpus' `ui-profile-root-positive` document (own layout root with options, appearance and a new-screens default, own application-scope screen template root, blueprint root with a version pin) is translated by Stage's converter and resolved to an effective shell by the .NET Scene engine and by the TypeScript engine against the same checked-in expectations (`Source/Conformance.Specs/for_UiProfileRootVector`, `Source/Frontend/src/uiProfileRootVector.spec.ts`).
- **Stage** ([Release v4.134.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.134.0)): Screen templates that declare `scopes application` and fit no slot carry the root role, so Scene's resolver accepts them as the application's shell, as Screenplay's validator already does.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.134.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.134.0)): Bundles Scene 4.67.0 and Screenplay 4.201.0; frozen digests refreshed.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.133.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.133.0)): Stage's specification runner now runs specifications for concepts that use `generate rule` (ESM v21) when the generated value is pinned. Like the reference evaluator, it checks the pinned String against the concept's rules and never runs the generation body; unpinned generation stays unsupported. A model whose `generate rule` concepts are used by no command is no longer refused. (part of #414)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.133.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.133.0)): The Host refuses only the commands that generate a `generate rule` value, with `STAGE-ESM-028` naming the command, property and concept, because its interpreter never runs implementation bodies; render the application to run them. Rendering still refuses `generate rule` with `STAGE-ESM-028`. (part of #414)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.132.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.132.0)): A protected live collection over a multi-block projection renders. The refusal named Chronicle#4816, which is fixed in the pinned Chronicle.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.132.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.132.0)): Running totals (`add`, `subtract`, `increment`, `decrement`) establish a required whole number from nothing, so a projection that only counts is complete. An optional whole number still cannot accumulate.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.132.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.132.0)): The live blueprint scenario now covers all eight samples (Orders added), runs them against the published blueprint packages 4.66.0, and fails instead of noting a template the package does not ship.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.132.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.132.0)): Cratis.Scene.Engine and Cratis.Scene.Model 4.66.0.
- **AI** ([Release v7.66.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.66.0)): The Screenplay pin and the byte-identical compiler contract are 4.201.0.
- **AI** ([Release v7.66.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.66.0)): The profile examples name the shell with `root template` instead of the legacy `layout` line, which now warns PLAY0791 and joins the preferred-syntax style codes.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.74.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.74.0)): Resolved SQL identities are applied each time a connection opens, so rotated passwords and refreshed access tokens take effect without a restart. A failed login drops the cached resolution and is retried. (part of #4948)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.74.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.74.0)): Requires Cratis Arc 22.109.0 or later. (part of #4948)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.202.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.202.0)): `Samples/Crm`, `Samples/Ticketing` and `Samples/Inventory`: `CurrentFigures` is an `observable` list query over the one `global` figures row instead of an `observable ... optional by summaryId` lookup. A keyed live lookup is not something the generated application can serve, and a one-row live list keeps the dashboard live without changing what it shows. The conformance syntax vectors of those three samples follow.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.202.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.202.0)): `Samples/Crm/README.md` says so and counts the blueprint's twenty screen templates (Cratis/Scene#181).
- **Stage** ([Release v4.131.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.131.0)): Commands that route their events to an event stream can now also return a response, including a generated value: Stage runs and renders them in the Host, the specification runner and generated code, and the generated TypeScript proxy carries the response type. STAGE-ESM-038 no longer refuses commands for combining routing, responses and generated values. (#451)
- **cli** ([Release v3.70.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.70.0)): Bundles Stage 4.130.0 (renders a ui profile's `root template`, shell `option`s and `appearance`, Cratis/Stage#466) and Screenplay 4.200.0 (root template language and diagnostics `PLAY0790`-`PLAY0799`).
- **cli** ([Release v3.70.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.70.0)): `cratis run` reports the compiler's root template errors `PLAY0790`-`PLAY0799` with their code and `file(line,column)` before Docker starts, and a version pin no installed blueprint satisfies - and any other root template that cannot be the shell - as the new `CLI-RENDER-019`, which `cratis render` reports too.
- **cli** ([Release v3.70.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.70.0)): The `Integration/Cli/Shell` fixture roots its profile on an own template with `option showFooter false` and a dark `appearance` with an accent; the CI script runs it on a live Stage twice (own root and the default blueprint's root), asserts the shell options and appearance in the browser, and checks that `cratis run` and `cratis render` refuse an unsatisfied pin.
- **cli** ([Release v3.70.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.70.0)): Documentation for the root template in `run.md` and `screenplay.md`.
- **Scene** ([Release v4.67.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.67.0)): A navigation item authored with `slot <name>`, where `<name>` is an outlet the shell declares (not a nested `core:outlet` of a screen), renders its target into that slot: `ApplicationHost` hosts the outlet (`CompositionOptions.hostedOutlets`), `placeInOutlet` keeps the primary screen and places the target beside it (`shellOutletOwner`), and both items are active.
- **Scene** ([Release v4.67.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.67.0)): The default `AppShell` draws any slot beyond its own regions as a side pane; a hosted outlet has a `Close` button backed by the new `closeOutlet(name)` on the navigation context.
- **Scene** ([Release v4.67.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.67.0)): Documentation in `preview-host.md`.
- **AI** ([Release v7.65.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.65.0)): The Stage skills separate what Stage runs from what it renders. Since Stage 4.129.0, the specification runner and host run protected command reads: a missing read is rejected first, and `require` and produced values use the read's properties. Rendering protected reads is still refused with STAGE-ESM-044. A protected command with a requirement below Error severity is refused with STAGE-ESM-045, and the host refuses outside appends while it decides a protected command. (#739)
- **AI** ([Release v7.65.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.65.0)): The Stage skills teach the opt-in for rendering reducers as self-reducing read models in new applications (Stage 4.128.0), and the root-template shell guidance follows Stage 4.130.0. (#739)
- **AI** ([Release v7.65.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.65.0)): The generated-values skills teach that a `generate rule` body's generation context is a language-neutral contract (`Now`, `Tenant`, a String result) that each target binds to its own type, with the Cratis C# binding to Fundamentals' `GenerationContext`. The legacy-extraction skill recovers compound rule bodies as far as the Arc generator does. (#721)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.201.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.201.0)): The canonical `screen-composition/v1` corpus carries the ui profile root template vectors (the `uiprofile-root-*` set of the Scene shared fixtures): `ui-profile-root-positive` (own layout, own application-scope screen template and blueprint template with a version pin as the root template, shell options, appearance, new screens default), `ui-profile-root-legacy-layout`, and the rejection vectors `ui-profile-root-unknown` (PLAY0790, PLAY0792, PLAY0798), `ui-profile-root-not-a-shell` (PLAY0794, PLAY0795, PLAY0799) and `ui-profile-root-invalid` (PLAY0796, PLAY0797), with their exact diagnostics pinned. Every vector prints canonically, compiles again and round trips through syntax JSON losslessly in C#; the TypeScript compiler reads them all and reports the legacy `layout` line (`PLAY0791`) like the C# compiler does. Docs: the ui profile page describes the conversion contract and the corpus (#827)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.201.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.201.0)): MCP: the new read tool `ui-profiles` returns each ui profile's root template with its pin and options, the appearance, the new screens default, the legacy layout line and the shell diagnostics, plus the own templates applicable as the root template or the new screens default and the accepted appearance values. The compiler's own rules back it (`ProfileShellValidator`), so a refused edit and the read agree. The edits are the typed nodes `RootTemplateSyntax`, `ShellOptionSyntax`, `ProfileAppearanceSyntax` and `NewScreenTemplateSyntax` through `read-ast`/`propose-ast`, which refuse an invalid reference with its PLAY0790-PLAY0799 code. The `when_authoring_the_ui_profile_shell` transcript runs on the corpus vector; tool reference, authoring guide and the contract golden are updated (part of #828)
- **AI** ([Release v7.64.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.64.0)): The Screenplay compiler pin is 4.199.0.
- **Scene** ([Release v4.66.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.66.0)): `resolveTemplateReference` (TypeScript) and `TemplateReferenceResolver.Resolve` (C#) answer from the names the blueprint's manifest lists (`screenTemplates`, `dialogTemplates`, `layouts`) when its source carries no template bodies, with the same `Resolved` kind and the same `Blueprint '<name>' has no template '<id>'` diagnostic otherwise. A source that carries its templates is answered from them, as before.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.130.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.130.0)): The converter carries a ui profile's `root` template (own or blueprint, with the version pin and shell options), `appearance` (colour scheme, accent) and `defaults` (new-screens template) into the Scene document the Stage serves, and the render planner asks Scene's `ShellResolver` (through `RootShell.Resolve`) what the root resolves to, so an unknown root, a blueprint that is not installed or a pin no installed version satisfies is a build-time finding (`RenderFindings`).
- **Stage** ([Release v4.130.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.130.0)): The Stage runtime starts the shell `resolveEffectiveShell` names (the function the Studio preview uses), applies the profile's shell options (`navigationMode`, `menuTheme`, `showFooter`) and appearance (light/dark/system scheme, accent as the theme's primary colour), and states an unresolved root or an option it does not understand instead of drawing another shell or a blank page.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.130.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.130.0)): Scene 4.53.1 -> 4.62.0 and Screenplay 4.188/4.193 -> 4.197.0; frozen digests and pins refreshed; documentation in `reference/stage-rendering.md`.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.129.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.129.0)): While a protected command is being decided, the host refuses any event appended to the session's event log from outside, rather than only events that affect the command's reads. (part of #440)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.129.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.129.0)): A protected command with a requirement below Error severity is refused with STAGE-ESM-045, also when a reaction invokes it, until Screenplay defines how such requirements behave. (part of #440)
- **Scene** ([Release v4.65.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.65.0)): `blueprintCatalog.json` (package and repository root) lists the two new templates; the CRM documentation counts them.
- **AI** ([Release v7.63.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.63.0)): The `matches` rule is taught as Screenplay's portable match dialect, including its anchors, character classes, whitespace set and PLAY0699 refusals, instead of as a platform regular expression. (#723)
- **AI** ([Release v7.63.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.63.0)): Stage guidance states what Stage 4.127.0 admits (ESM v1–v21 and v23–v25), what it refuses (v22, described handlers, atomic batches, protected reads) and which generated-value strategies it renders. Statements that described handlers never bind or that every read is unprotected are corrected.
- **AI** ([Release v7.63.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.63.0)): The Screenplay compiler pin is 4.198.0.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.200.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.200.0)): Root template diagnostics `PLAY0790`-`PLAY0799` (unknown template, legacy `layout`, unknown blueprint, unsatisfied version pin, template not applicable as shell, no content outlet, unknown shell option, invalid appearance, duplicate root template, new-screens template not applicable to a feature). One rule set, `ProfileShellValidator`, run by the compiler (document facts) and by `UiProfileShellBinder.Bind` (installed blueprint catalog facts); the rules restate Scene's `ShellResolver` / `TemplateApplicability` shared vectors. (#826)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.200.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.200.0)): The TypeScript compiler reports the legacy profile `layout` line (`PLAY0791`, shared `diagnostics.json` vectors hold C# and TS to the same code/line) and `QuickFixes` offers the mechanical fix-it: `layout X` becomes `root template X`, or the ignored `layout` line is removed beside a root template. Monaco and VS Code code actions pick it up. (#826)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.200.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.200.0)): Contract golden regenerated (the new codes and their arguments); docs: diagnostics table, ui-profile page.
- **Cast** ([Release v0.2.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Cast/releases/tag/v0.2.0)): Tiers on member pages always come from the registry; a member's `cast.json` cannot change a tier, and profiles for another login are ignored (#10)
- **Cast** ([Release v0.2.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Cast/releases/tag/v0.2.0)): A member's profile that cannot be read or rendered falls back to the GitHub profile instead of stopping the directory build (#10)
- **AI** ([Release v7.62.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.62.0)): Arc C# guidance starts new code with read protection `On` (`<CratisReadProtection>On</CratisReadProtection>`): the state rule goes in a `CommandValidator<T>` with the read model as a constructor parameter, and `Handle()` returns the events. Under `Off`, the default, a validator over a plain read is a hint. `[ProtectedDecision]` with `DecisionRead<T>` is described as the older per-command opt-in, not obsolete (#731)
- **AI** ([Release v7.62.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.62.0)): The "choosing how a rule holds" reference is rewritten around the rule shapes (unique value, one entity, two entities with `[LookUpBy]`, per-stream read model, stream decision, aggregate, explicit scope, repair reactor and `[Unprotected("why")]`), with the refusals, the Measure rollout and how to test the race (#731)
- **AI** ([Release v7.62.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.62.0)): C# specification guidance uses a plain `new CommandScenario<T>()`, `AppendConcurrently` and `ShouldHaveValidationErrorBecauseOf(ValidationResultReason.ConcurrencyViolation)`; `UseDecisionReads()` is no longer taught (#731)
- **AI** ([Release v7.62.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.62.0)): Reducer guidance states that unfiltered reducers without constructor dependencies are admitted by decision reads and read protection (#731)
- **AI** ([Release v7.62.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.62.0)): Evaluation cases cover the rule shapes, the Measure rollout, `ReadChanged` handling, the race test and the negative case of a validator under `Off` (#731)
- **AI** ([Release v7.62.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.62.0)): Validator guidance states that a caller sending `X-Allowed-Severity: Error` can remove validator Error results under `Off` and `Measure` unless the command declares `[BlockOnValidationSeverity]`, and that from Arc 22.108.0 a command declared `On` blocks `Error` and `Unknown` whatever the caller sends (#731)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.127.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.127.1)): Stage's specification runner and fixture checks now evaluate concept and command value rules, including `validate checksum`, with Screenplay's public value-rule check (the same implementation as the reference evaluator) instead of Stage's own copies. Results are unchanged; rejection messages for deprecated Studio lookaround patterns now use Screenplay's wording. Generated applications are unchanged. (#455)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.109.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.109.0)): `EntityFrameworkCoreOptions` (`ConnectionString`, `AutoDiscoverDbContexts`, `ConnectionResolution`) now bind from the `Cratis:EntityFrameworkCore` configuration section. The `configureOptions` callback runs afterwards and wins. (part of #3316)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.73.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.73.1)): The namespace selector, breadcrumb and home page no longer depend on shared MVVM singleton state; the last chosen namespace per event store is remembered in the browser for event store level pages.
- **Arc** ([Release v22.108.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.108.0)): `ShouldAdmitDecisionReadsOf` and `ShouldAdmitDecisionReadsIn` also check commands declared `On`, the read models their validators take, and per-stream read models (#3330)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.127.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.127.0)): A model that declares protected command reads (`command reads protected`) is now refused with STAGE-ESM-044 by the renderer, the specification runner, the host and the default sandbox engine, including when only part of the model is rendered, instead of running or rendering the commands without their reads. (part of #440)
- **cli** ([Release v3.69.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.69.0)): The CLI bundles Stage 4.125.0, whose catalog ships `PrimeReact` and `Cratis.Blueprint.Default` and whose live Stage and generated React application load the templates of approved domain blueprints; `cratis run` starts `cratis/stage:4.125.0` by default. Scene 4.53.1 accepts a blueprint manifest in the approved package set.
- **cli** ([Release v3.69.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.69.0)): Two scope specs that used a generated-concept collision as their example of a construct Stage refuses now use an interval reaction (`STAGE-ESM-024`), since Stage 4.121 onward renders the former.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.198.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.198.0)): The Library, Commerce and TimeTracking samples declare `reaction batches atomic` at their root, so a model copied from them starts with all-or-none reaction production batches. Invoicing already did. (#771)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.198.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.198.0)): The getting-started walk-through, the MCP authoring guide and the `open-workspace` tool description tell new models to declare `reaction batches atomic`, and present per-fact production as legacy behavior kept for existing models. See [Atomic production batches](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/blob/main/Documentation/screenplay/reactions.md#atomic-production-batches). (#771)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.197.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.197.0)): `ui profile` gains `root template [blueprint <Blueprint>] <Name> [version "<range>"]` with `option <name> <value>` lines, an `appearance` block (`color scheme light|dark|system`, `accent "<#hex>"`) and `new screens use template <ref>`. `layout <Name>` stays readable beside it.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.197.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.197.0)): New syntax nodes `RootTemplateSyntax`, `ShellOptionSyntax`, `ProfileAppearanceSyntax`, `NewScreenTemplateSyntax`; parser (`ProfileShellParser`), printer (byte-identical round trip), walker, transport schema (C# and TS generated files), docs (grammar, ui-profile, templates, diagnostics), Monaco completion/keyword docs, VS Code TextMate grammar, Invoicing sample.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.197.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.197.0)): Diagnostics PLAY0788 (malformed/repeated shell lines), PLAY0789 (malformed/repeated appearance), PLAY0798 (second root template); a bad version range reuses PLAY0721.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.197.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.197.0)): Template renames stay refused (layouts/templates are not rename targets), so a profile's reference cannot go stale; folder merge needs no change (a profile lives in one file).
- **Scene** ([Release v4.62.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.62.0)): `composeApplicationScreen` (preview host and Stage's composition) resolves the shell through the engine's `resolveEffectiveShell` (`effectiveShellOf`) instead of `shellComponentForLayout(screen.layout) ?? screen.layout`; the template chain's placement and the outlet a screen lands in are the resolved shell's outlets / primary outlet. With a profile `root`, that template is the shell of every screen (a screen may still opt out with the chrome-less `FullPage` layout); without one, behaviour is unchanged (legacy layout).
- **Scene** ([Release v4.62.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.62.0)): A root screen template (role Root) is drawn by the template component with its own arrangement/content; the screen goes in its primary outlet.
- **Scene** ([Release v4.62.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.62.0)): `previewProfile` now carries `root`, `appearance` and `defaults`.
- **Scene** ([Release v4.62.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.62.0)): `CompositionOptions.templateLibrary` lets a host hand over the installed blueprints.
- **Scene** ([Release v4.62.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.62.0)): Docs: `blueprints/preview-host.md`.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.126.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.126.0)): Commands that route their events to an event stream can now also have generated properties: Stage runs and renders them in the Host, the specification runner and generated code, including a generated identifier as the event source of a routed stream. Generated commands that return results now check their stream id the same way as other routed commands. A route combined with a command response is still refused with STAGE-ESM-038. (part of #451)
- **Scene** ([Release v4.61.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.61.0)): `TemplateMetadata` gains `role` and `primaryOutlet`; `ApplicableTemplates` default scopes treat a root screen template as application-scope.
- **Scene** ([Release v4.60.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.60.0)): Storybook story `Blueprint/Gallery navigation` (click an entry, select mode, destinations list) for the SC4 navigating shell controls.
- **Scene** ([Release v4.60.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.60.0)): `locationTrail` and `useShellLink` are exported from the shell barrel, so hosts and other blueprints can reuse them.
- **Templates** ([Release v1.8.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Templates/releases/tag/v1.8.0)): New C# applications from the `cratis` and `cratis-aspire` templates start with Arc read protection `On`, so read models that commands and validators take guard the command's append. The README explains how to switch to `Measure` or `Off` (#80)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.73.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.73.0)): Application read model databases are not dropped by the removal; the application owns that data.
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.31.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.31.0)): AuthProxy now refuses to start when `Session:CookieDomain` is combined with the `SubHost` tenant resolution, when a declared service host or the callback host is outside the cookie domain, when the domain is an address, a top-level domain, a wildcard or carries a port, when the cookie name is not a valid name, and when the name uses the `__Host-` prefix together with a domain. None of these can occur unless you set the new options.
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.31.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.31.0)): Invitations, credential links and registration still run on the host they start on. With a callback host, register a provider callback for every host where one of them can start.
- **Scene** ([Release v4.58.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.58.0)): **Shell controls navigate through the navigation context** (spec N1, N2): `menuItem`, `breadcrumb` crumbs, `logo`, `userMenu` entries and the topbar/sidebar region items activate via the new `useNavigationActivation` (`@cratis/scene.react`). A click renders the target in its slot and records it in the host history; the address of the hosting page is never written. Outside a host they stay plain hash links.
- **Scene** ([Release v4.58.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.58.0)): **Active state is derived from the history location** (N1): a menu entry is current while its screen is the primary screen (`useShellLink`), no document-kept `isActive` needed.
- **Scene** ([Release v4.58.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.58.0)): **The breadcrumb follows the location** (N4): `locationTrail` derives module › feature trail from the structure; default application chrome now includes a breadcrumb; authored `items` still win.
- **Scene** ([Release v4.58.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.58.0)): **Activation policy** (N1/N2): `NavigationActivationProvider`, `navigationMode` (`navigate` | `select` | per-control function), Alt-click selects, `onActivate`, `onNavigate`; `ApplicationHost` takes `navigationPolicy`.
- **Scene** ([Release v4.58.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.58.0)): **`ScenePreviewHost`**: new `{ application }` target, `initialDestination` (screen or dialog), `navigationMode`, `onActivate`, `onNavigate`.
- **Scene** ([Release v4.58.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.58.0)): **`listDestinations(document)`** (N3/N5): Pages / States / Dialogs for the *Showing* picker; `galleryApplication()` + `galleryComposition` make the blueprint gallery a navigable application. Listing pinned by a JSON fixture.
- **Scene** ([Release v4.58.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.58.0)): Docs: `Documentation/blueprints/preview-host.md`.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.125.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.125.0)): Bumped `Cratis.Scene.Engine`, `Cratis.Scene.Model` and the `@cratis/scene.*` packages to 4.53.1, which re-pins the digests of the generated frontend bytes.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.125.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.125.0)): `CratisRendering.ShippedPackages` is composed from the generated manifests; `ShippedPackageNames` has five entries, so an approved package may not reuse `PrimeReact` or `Cratis.Blueprint.Default`.
- **Scene** ([Release v4.57.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.57.0)): `UiProfile.Layout` is documented as legacy: it stays readable and writable, `Root` supersedes it. Existing documents without the new members load and write back unchanged.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.124.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.124.0)): `matches` validation rules now give the same result as Screenplay 4.188 everywhere. The specification runner and the host use Screenplay's own pattern matching, and generated validators match characters the same way (for example `.` no longer matches a line separator); a pattern that generated code cannot express exactly is refused with STAGE-ESM-005 instead of being rendered with different behavior. (#448)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.124.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.124.0)): The default sandbox engine (`cratis run` without the semantic engine) does not enforce the four new text rules; they are added to its documented list of rules it does not enforce. (#448)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.18.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.18.0)): The generic `IJobsManager.Start` and `GetJobsOfType` methods now document that they are not safe while two releases share a cluster; use `IJobs` instead. (#76)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.123.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.123.0)): Commands whose generated property names clash with names the Cratis renderer uses inside the generated `Handle` method (such as `operation`, `occurred` or `source`) now render: the renderer renames its own locals instead of refusing the model with STAGE-ESM-038, and the Stage Host and specification runner no longer refuse these models. Generated code is unchanged for commands without such clashes. (part of #451)
- **AI** ([Release v7.60.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.60.1)): `module-navigation-modes-and-slice-buttons` and `blueprint-template-and-sample-data` tell the agent there is no model file and to write the declarations from scratch. Before, the blueprint task ended with the agent asking for the repository (the skill was never loaded).
- **AI** ([Release v7.60.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.60.1)): The two navigation assertions of the first task are worded as what the answer declares (Sales `extends`, Support `overrides`, Reports nothing; each item targets its own module), not as "lists only its own item", which graders read both ways for an `extends` region.
- **AI** ([Release v7.60.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.60.0)): A query never writes `Skip`, `Take`, `Limit` or `OrderBy` to serve the client's page or sort. The old advice to give every list a default `OrderBy` is removed; an initial order is the frontend's `sorting` argument to `useWithPaging`.
- **AI** ([Release v7.60.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.60.0)): The `AsQueryable()` examples in the paging skill and `vertical-slices.md` now show `Observe`, and `IQueryable<T>` is described as the shape for LINQ-native providers such as Entity Framework Core.
- **AI** ([Release v7.60.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.60.0)): The React, code review, performance review and read-model query guidance, and the performance reviewer agent, no longer present `IQueryable<T>` as the only paged shape or `Skip`/`Take` as how a query pages.
- **AI** ([Release v7.60.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.60.0)): The paging skill documents the `Expression not supported ... Skip(0).Take(30).Cast()` failure: Arc before 22.50.6 appended a `Cast` that MongoDB cannot translate to every paged `IQueryable` request.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.122.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.122.0)): A model that describes a command handler without supplying its implementation is now refused with STAGE-IMPL-002 by the renderer, the specification runner and the host, instead of being rendered or run without the handler. (part of #316)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.122.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.122.0)): A model that declares atomic reaction batches (`reaction batches atomic`) is now refused with STAGE-ESM-043 on every path until Stage supports them. (part of #316)
- **cli** ([Release v3.68.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.68.0)): The CLI bundles Stage 4.120.0 and Screenplay 4.188.0 (including its MCP server); `cratis run` starts `cratis/stage:4.120.0` by default.
- **AI** ([Release v7.58.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.58.0)): The pinned Screenplay moves from 4.164.0 to 4.188.0 with its compiler contract. The `translate-example`, `invoicing-dues-example` and `invoicing-dues-design` examples keep the capture in the `Translate` slice and put the translator reactions in an `Automation` slice beside it (a reaction in the capture's own directionless slice is `PLAY0694`), and append the first record of a key with `when added`, so they compile and run clean again.
- **AI** ([Release v7.58.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.58.0)): The `preferred-syntax` spec counts ten non-error style diagnostics, now including `PLAY0787`.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.121.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.121.0)): Generated `Common` concept, type and enum files now use short type names with `using` directives instead of `global::`-qualified names. The usings sit inside the file-scoped namespace and are rooted with `global::`, so code you add in `Customizations/` cannot change what a generated name refers to; names that cannot be proven safe stay qualified. Existing generated applications see a one-time text change in these files on their next regeneration; other generated files follow in later releases. (part of #424)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.121.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.121.0)): The rendered scaffold's `.editorconfig` places `using` directives inside the namespace for generated code. (part of #424)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.121.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.121.0)): Do not declare your own types inside generated namespaces; see https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/blob/main/Documentation/guides/customize-rendered-application.md. (part of #424)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.188.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.188.0)): The `propose-ast` tool description names the application shell edits and their diagnostics; the capability matrix, the release acceptance matrix and the MCP guides gain the new rows.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.120.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.120.0)): Commands with generated properties can now also have command rules and requirements: Stage runs and renders them in the Host, the specification runner and generated code, checking the requirements on the request values before any value is generated, as Screenplay does. STAGE-ESM-038 is now reported only when a rule or requirement reads a generated property. (part of #451)
- **cli** ([Release v3.67.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.67.0)): The CLI bundles Stage 4.119.1 (built on Screenplay 4.180.1); `cratis run` starts `cratis/stage:4.119.1` by default. Generated applications pin Arc 22.104.0, and validation rules of every severity on generated concepts now render instead of being refused with `STAGE-ESM-038` (an unpinned generated value Stage cannot prove still is).
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.184.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.184.0)): `matches` patterns in the exact shapes Studio generated for equals, not equals, contains, not contains, starts with and ends with (both the `$` and the `(?![\s\S])` forms) now bind as the matching first-class rule with warning PLAY0787 and a quick fix, instead of failing with PLAY0699. Executable models already holding these patterns evaluate them as the first-class rule. Every other lookaround is still refused. (#810)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.119.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.119.0)): STAGE-ESM-028 now refuses only `generate rule` among the ESM v21 generation strategies. (part of #414)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.118.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.118.0)): STAGE-ESM-042 is now reported only for concept rule kinds Stage does not know; the legacy event-model engine still refuses checksum rules. (part of #414)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.117.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.117.0)): Stage and the rendered backend scaffold use Arc 22.104.0 (with Cratis.Fundamentals 7.25.0). Generated values that fail a concept rule of any severity are now rejected by the rendered application at run time. (part of #414)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.117.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.117.0)): Generated values guarded by warning- or information-severity concept rules now render instead of being refused with STAGE-ESM-038; concepts with unproven or refuted rules still need pinned fixture values. (part of #414)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.183.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.183.0)): A `matches` pattern is now a flagless JavaScript `RegExp` restricted to a portable linear dialect. Both compilers refuse lookarounds, backreferences, `\b`, `\p{…}`, .NET-only syntax (`\A`, `\z`, inline options, class subtraction) and patterns over the size budget with PLAY0699. The message names the construct and suggests an equivalent where one exists. Existing models that use one of these constructs no longer compile: rewrite the pattern as the message suggests, for example `\A`/`\z` as `^`/`$` or a length-only lookahead as a counted repeat. See [Commands](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/blob/main/Documentation/screenplay/commands.md). (#800)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.183.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.183.0)): `matches` rules are evaluated by Screenplay's own linear-time matcher instead of .NET's regular expression engine. Verdicts no longer depend on how busy the host is, on the target framework or on runtime settings, and there is no timeout. (#800)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.183.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.183.0)): A few `matches` verdicts in the reference evaluator now agree with JavaScript where they used to differ: `\s` no longer matches Unicode spaces such as the no-break space U+00A0 (this also affects the built-in `email` rule), `.` no longer matches `\r`, U+2028 or U+2029, and `\w` no longer matches U+0130. (#800)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.183.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.183.0)): Executable models saved by earlier versions still load when a pattern is outside the dialect. The evaluator reports such a rule as an unsupported validation, and generation checks report it as Unproven instead of timing out. (#800)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.182.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.182.0)): A pending handler intent without `produces` now fails executable binding with PLAY0696 instead of PLAY0268. Its source stays valid. (#782)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.106.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.106.0)): `new CommandScenario<T>()` enables decision reads automatically when the command needs them; `UseDecisionReads()` is no longer needed (#3276)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.106.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.106.0)): Commands routed through event source definitions pass their declared routing to Chronicle under `On`, so the append is checked against the definition's concurrency (#3283)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.106.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.106.0)): ARCCHR0002, ARCCHR0011, ARCCHR0012 and ARCCHR0013 follow the read-protection rules, and `[LookUpBy]` lookups are checked against the command a validator belongs to (#3268)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.106.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.106.0)): Requires Chronicle 19.
- **Arc** ([Release v22.105.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.105.0)): The Screenplay generator now states reactors that return several events whose append constraints could reject the batch, by declaring `reaction batches atomic` (Screenplay ESM v22). Chronicle appends such a batch all or nothing, and the generated model now says the same. Batches that close a stream, and batches whose rejecting constraint is not in the generated document (such as a class-level `[Unique]`), keep their code reference with SP0070. With `MaximumExecutableModelVersion` below 22 the output is unchanged. Arc now depends on Cratis.Screenplay 4.181.0. See [Generating a Screenplay](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/blob/main/Documentation/backend/csharp/generating-a-screenplay.md). (#3093)
- **Scene** ([Release v4.42.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.42.0)): Nothing is renamed. The spec names stand beside the earlier templates, which keep working: `LoginScreen` beside `Login`, `NotFoundScreen` beside `NotFound`, `ForbiddenScreen` beside `AccessDenied`, `ErrorScreen` beside `Error`, `QuickCreateDialog` beside `FormDialog` (`foundationTemplateAliases`). The `ConfirmDialog` of the components blueprint and this one are kept apart because a template reference names its blueprint.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.115.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.115.0)): A rendered specification may leave a generated value unpinned when every rule on its concept is proven by Screenplay's rule consistency check; unproven or refuted rules still require a pin (`STAGE-ESM-038`). (#423)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.181.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.181.1)): The version-1 generation context for `generate rule` bodies is a language-neutral contract (`Now`, `Tenant`, String result) that the selected provider binds to its own type.
