- **Stage** ([Release v4.87.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.87.0)): Stage pins Arc 22.83.0 and Screenplay 4.147.1 and admits ESM v1–v15. Models below v15 render and run as before. (#357)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.87.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.87.0)): At v15, a pinned generated fixture must match its strategy's shape. An implicit UUID pin must be a lowercase version-4 UUID, so a pin such as `11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111` fails `STAGE-ESM-011`. (#357)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.87.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.87.0)): Unknown generation strategies fail `STAGE-ESM-028`, and the legacy syntax engine refuses every explicit strategy with `STAGE-ESM-028`. Slices with several commands and conditional productions remain refused (`STAGE-ESM-004`, `STAGE-ESM-006`), tracked in https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/issues/360. (#357)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.54.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.54.0)): Custom `IReadModels` implementations keep compiling: `Observe<T>()` has a default implementation that throws `ReadModelCannotBeObserved`. Passive read models and read models that target an event sequence throw the same exception, because they have no stored collection to observe (#4759)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.86.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.86.0)): `STAGE-ESM-034` now refuses only `runs as` reactions that invoke commands whose authorization reads claims or uses opaque policies. Arc's system principal carries subject claims that Screenplay treats as unknown, so such a command could be allowed in the rendered application and denied by the model. (#292)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.86.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.86.0)): Reaction appends that fan out to several reactions, cascade further, use `$context`, expect read-model or query state, or produce events that constraints check still fail `STAGE-ESM-011`. (part of #207)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.85.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.85.0)): The Screenplay generator uses Cratis Screenplay 4.146.0 (#3196)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.148.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.148.0)): Feeding a returned value into another command's generated input still reports PLAY0485 (part of #300)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.148.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.148.0)): The Invoicing sample navigates to the registered invoice from a returned value, and the forms, interactions and commands documentation describe the continuation scope (part of #300)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.83.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.83.0)): Arc requires Cratis Fundamentals 7.24.0 or later (#3165)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.83.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.83.0)): `ARC0022` accepts concepts that implement `IGeneratableInContext<T>` (#3165)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.147.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.147.2)): `run-public-acceptance.sh` runs the Studio production vector with Studio's scratch proof (import the published corpus, edit and save a screen, export, Play) instead of the generic-selector harness, when `SCREENPLAY_STUDIO_CHECKOUT` points at a Studio checkout. The Studio URL defaults to the production principal-header proxy on `http://127.0.0.1:5156` when it answers, so the vector no longer reports `blocked-missing-studio-url` (part of #173).
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.147.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.147.2)): The blocked state names what to set (the URL, the checkout and the production proxy) instead of a stale deploy note.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.85.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.85.0)): Secret concepts that aren't text (`Int`, `Decimal`, `Bool`, `Date`, `DateTime`, `Uuid`) are refused with `STAGE-ESM-041`, both when rendering and in the Host, because Chronicle can't yet store them (see https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/issues/4813). `pii secret` concepts use personal-data protection and are not affected. (part of #346)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.84.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.84.0)): Stage uses Screenplay 4.146.0 and admits ESM v1–v14. Models without filters render exactly as before. (#348)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.84.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.84.0)): Filters on snapshot collection queries or DateTime properties fail `STAGE-ESM-010`. Filter parameter names that collide with other generated names fail `STAGE-ESM-012`. The specification executor refuses filtered query assertions with `STAGE-ESM-010` instead of ignoring the filters. (#348)
- **cli** ([Release v3.56.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.56.0)): The CLI bundles Stage 4.83.0 (from 4.80.0), so `cratis run` defaults to the `cratis/stage:4.83.0` image and `cratis render` plans with the Stage 4.83.0 renderer. The packed-CLI check now also runs Stage's responsive-shell assertions against the rendered application, which keeps the narrow-window menu working in what the CLI renders. (part of #284)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.147.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.147.0)): Generated values in specifications can be text, not only UUIDs (part of #616)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.147.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.147.0)): Decision 0054 records the delivery phases of #616 and the encoding details fixed at admission (part of #616)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.83.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.83.0)): The Scene packages are pinned at 4.22.1 (from 4.21.0) in the Stage frontend, the Host and the generated frontend package set.
- **Arc** ([Release v22.81.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.81.0)): Completing a stream from a command now closes it only for the command's event source, so another event source using the same stream type and stream id stays open. A command without a key property or `ICanProvideEventSourceId` still closes the stream for every event source. Completing a stream from a reactor closes it for the observed event's event source. Owner-qualified stream id templates such as `{Owner}:{Month}` are no longer needed to keep a close from reaching other event sources. Requires Chronicle 19.39 or later; an older Chronicle server fails the completion instead of closing the stream for every event source (#3126)
- **cli** ([Release v3.55.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.55.0)): The CLI bundles Stage 4.80.0 (from 4.79.1), the release whose live Stage loads approved packages at runtime, so `cratis run` defaults to the `cratis/stage:4.80.0` image.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.82.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.82.0)): Rendered backends and Stage itself pin Arc 22.78.0, including the Cratis metapackage. (part of #175)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.82.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.82.0)): Generated TypeScript proxies for commands with a response are typed with it, for example `Command<IRegisterProject, Guid>` for a scalar UUID response or `Command<IRegisterReceipt, RegisterReceiptResponse>` for a named one, instead of exposing the event wrapper. (part of #175)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.82.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.82.0)): `STAGE-ESM-037` now explains why reactions that both produce events and invoke commands are still refused. A refused command would make Chronicle redeliver the trigger and append the reaction's events again. The refusal stays until reactor appends are idempotent across redelivery (https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/issues/4809, https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/issues/3207). (part of #342)
- **AI** ([Release v7.39.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.39.0)): Commands that own their event use inline `produces event <Name>`, which defaults its destination to the command identifier; shared events keep a standalone `event` and use `produces <Name>` with an explicit `for`
- **AI** ([Release v7.39.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.39.0)): Compliance markers are written as bare `pii` and `secret` throughout
- **AI** ([Release v7.39.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.39.0)): The standalone Screenplay pin moves to 4.145.0 with its published contract
- **cli** ([Release v3.54.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.54.0)): Bundles Stage 4.79.1 and Screenplay 4.145.1, and runs `cratis/stage:4.79.1` by default. Server-generated UUID values, scalar and named command responses, and event-triggered reactions now render instead of being refused.
- **cli** ([Release v3.54.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.54.0)): `cratis ai install` and `cratis ai update` link `.github/instructions` to the corpus's generated Copilot adapters (`.cratis/ai/harnesses/copilot/instructions`), so Copilot applies the Cratis rules by path. An older corpus without those adapters keeps the link to `.cratis/ai/rules`, and `cratis ai update` relinks existing installs. (#341)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.81.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.81.0)): Event-triggered reactions no longer fail `STAGE-ESM-024` in the spec runner. Clock, application, startup and shutdown triggers and opaque reaction bodies still do, and a reaction mapping that reads the occurrence time without a `given clock` is rejected with the reference's message. (part of #207)
- **AI** ([Release v7.38.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.38.2)): The screens smoke pins the current public vector: CLI 3.53.1 and `Cratis.Screenplay.CanonicalCorpus` 4.145.0, so the MCP edit and render replay on the vector that carries history, guarded actions, toolbar and module navigation (part of Cratis/AI#536).
- **AI** ([Release v7.38.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.38.2)): The screens authoring workflow and UI-composition skills say the browser run passes on CLI 3.53.1 and that it now runs in corpus CI.
- **Scene** ([Release v4.22.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.22.0)): `chart` describes `data` (labels, datasets), `options` and `style`; `treeTable` describes `columns`, `items` (nodes down four levels, a node's `data` a map) and `selection`; `dataScroller` `items` and `multiStateCheckbox` `options`, `icons` and `value` are described. Structured values stay bindable to a data context, a query result or a component property.
- **AI** ([Release v7.38.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.38.1)): The Stage rendering skill states that Stage 4.79.1 renders keyed lookups and filtered observable collections whose scalar key concept has validation rules, converting the key inside the query so an out-of-rule key finds no instance; earlier Stage releases, including the one bundled with cratis CLI 3.53.1, still refuse a validated snapshot-lookup key (#635)
- **AI** ([Release v7.38.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.38.0)): The standalone Screenplay pin moves to 4.144.0 with its published contract
- **Arc** ([Release v22.79.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.79.0)): For commands routed through an event source definition, appending a returned `EventForEventSourceId` whose legacy routing names a registered definition and a stream it does not declare now fails with `EventStreamDoesNotBelongToEventSource`, as typed routing already did, instead of appending to an undeclared stream. (part of #2987)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.79.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.79.0)): The public constructors of `SingleEventForEventSourceIdCommandResponseValueHandler`, `EventsForEventSourceIdCommandResponseValueHandler` and `EventsWithConcurrencyScopesCommandResponseValueHandler` that do not take `IEventSources` are obsolete; they skip routing validation. Dependency injection uses the new constructors. (part of #2987)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.79.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.79.0)): `STAGE-ESM-024` for event-triggered reactions now applies only to the specification executor, which still does not run reactions. Clock, application, startup and shutdown triggers and opaque reaction bodies still fail `STAGE-ESM-024` in both the Host and the specification executor. (part of #207)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.79.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.79.0)): The reaction rendering reference explains how the Host, rendered Chronicle reactors and the specification executor each handle event reactions: https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/blob/main/Documentation/reference/reaction-rendering.md (part of #207)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.53.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.53.0)): Analyzer `CHR0039` now reports a discarded awaitable `Should*` assertion from a Cratis testing surface as an **error** instead of a warning. Such an assertion throws on a `Task` nobody observes, so the spec passes whatever the code does. This can turn a test project's build red where it contains such vacuous specs: await each flagged assertion and make the containing member `async Task`. While migrating, you can temporarily downgrade the rule in your repository's `.editorconfig` with `dotnet_diagnostic.CHR0039.severity = warning`; see https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/blob/main/Documentation/code-analysis/CHR0039.mdx. (#4177)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.144.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.144.0)): `open-workspace` and `workspace-state` report the notes revision and whether the notes file is readable. A malformed or conflicted notes file never blocks opening a model; note tools refuse to write until it is fixed (#391)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.144.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.144.0)): Recovery journals that include notes are written as version 2, which older servers refuse instead of rolling back without the notes file (#391)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.52.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.52.0)): Conflicting explicit registrations, such as two different identifiers or generations for the same type, or two types sharing an identifier and generation, now fail at startup with `ConflictingArtifactDeclaration` or `ConflictingArtifactKind` instead of registering silently. (#4732)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.52.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.52.0)): A store registers nothing with the kernel until all of its artifacts have been prepared successfully. A preparation failure is reported through the registration outcome and is not retried as a connection failure. (#4732)
- **AI** ([Release v7.37.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.37.0)): The Templates guidance reflects Templates 1.7.4 (toaster placement and port defaults), and the Stage guidance explains semantic execution in Stage 4.78.0, which cratis CLI 3.53.1 does not expose yet (#460)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.143.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.143.1)): Browser history: selecting a work item and clearing the selection are history entries. Back restores the selection and its comments, forward clears it, and a refresh on the selected address restores the selection from the address alone.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.143.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.143.1)): Guarded actions on a freshly seeded work item (so no other assertion depends on its state): double click on an open row navigates, Close is offered and enabled only while the item is open and posts `CloseWorkItem` for it, the projection shows it closed, Close is hidden afterwards, and double click on the closed row notifies and stays on the list.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.143.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.143.1)): Against the installed CLI 3.53.1 and `cratis/stage:4.73.1` with the repository corpus the harness passes with no blockers; the 17 new assertions are `browser.history.*` and `browser.guarded.*`. (part of #173)
- **AI** ([Release v7.36.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.36.0)): Concept compliance (`pii`/`personal`, `secret`) is taught as binding in ESM v13 with the standalone compiler, with the remaining PLAY0268 refusals for enum and identity concepts and explicit `subject` marks, report-only reasons (PLAY0270), and the boundaries that cratis CLI 3.53.1 still refuses markers and Stage admits up to v12 (#621)
- **AI** ([Release v7.36.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.36.0)): The standalone Screenplay pin moves to 4.142.0 with its published contract
- **Stage** ([Release v4.78.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.78.0)): Clock, application, startup and shutdown triggers and opaque reaction bodies still fail `STAGE-ESM-024`, now naming the trigger kind; reactions that both produce and invoke, including different triggers on the same event, fail `STAGE-ESM-037`. (part of #207)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.78.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.78.0)): A command specification whose events would trigger a reaction fails `STAGE-ESM-011`, because the generated command specification does not settle reactions. (part of #207)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.143.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.143.0)): PLAY0268 now names the specific reason a query is not executable (a context-sourced filter, a filter on a single-result query, a filter without a matching scalar property, `scoped to` or `performer`) instead of one combined message (part of #140)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.143.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.143.0)): PLAY0468 reports unknown, repeated or missing required query arguments in both specification forms, and repeated query arguments are no longer silently dropped while parsing (part of #140)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.143.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.143.0)): The query and specification documentation states that collection results compare without regard to row order (part of #140)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.77.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.77.0)): The compliance rendering reference explains the mapping, the identity rule and its surrogate-identifier fix, and why concepts should be marked before their events are first published. Models without compliance render exactly as before.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.77.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.77.0)): Play and the Stage Host do not encrypt or redact values of personal-data concepts: the Host's own event store keeps them in plain text (see https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/issues/346).
- **AI** ([Release v7.35.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.35.0)): Synced with Arc 22.76.0, Chronicle 19.51.0, Fundamentals 7.23.0, Specifications 4.1.1, Orleans 1.16.3, Components 4.26.4, Chronicle.TypeScript 6.51.0, Arc.TypeScript 0.57.2, Chronicle.Kotlin 6.13.0, Arc.Kotlin 7.7.0, Chronicle.Elixir 3.10.2, cratis CLI 3.53.1 and Chronicle.Mcp 2.0.0; each skill's verified sources name the release read (#620)
- **AI** ([Release v7.35.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.35.0)): The standalone Screenplay pin moves to 4.141.0 with its published contract; Stage 4.74.0 to 4.76.0 capabilities are taught as standalone Stage, since cratis CLI 3.53.1 still bundles Stage 4.73.1
- **AI** ([Release v7.35.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.35.0)): The Chronicle MCP skill states the exact effect of each Chronicle.Mcp 2.0.0 tool, its read-only default and the mutation profile, and warns that it targets the active `cratis` context
- **AI** ([Release v7.35.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.35.0)): The CLI operations guard treats `observers remove` and `observers clear-partition-quarantine` as mutating commands
- **AI** ([Release v7.35.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.35.0)): Concurrency guidance teaches decision reads (`IStreamReads`, `[ProtectedDecision]` with `DecisionRead<T>`, `IDecisionReads`) instead of advisory read-model injection
- **AI** ([Release v7.35.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.35.0)): Scene packages are named `@cratis/scene.*` in the Stage scaffold tables

## Fixed

- **cli** ([Release v3.89.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.89.0)): `cratis update` shows the release notes after the CLI, template and Stage results, and builds include the notes of 3.87.0 and 3.88.0, which those releases did not keep. (part of #153)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.129.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.129.1)): Stopping a host whose read-protection measurement queue was already disposed, or stopping it twice, no longer fails shutdown with `ObjectDisposedException: The CancellationTokenSource has been disposed.` (#3442)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.90.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.90.1)): Changing only the `details` text of a `[ComplianceDetails]`/`[PII]` or `[Encrypted]` marking on an existing event type no longer counts as an event schema change, so the kernel no longer refuses the registration with `EventTypeSchemaChanged`. A change of the compliance metadata type or the encryption scope, or an added or removed marking, still counts as a schema change. (#4660)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.129.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.129.0)): Without a command transaction, events a command returns for one event source on several routes (streams, or a definition route next to a legacy route) are now appended as one batch that carries a guard for each route. A concurrency violation on any route now appends none of them, instead of leaving some appended. This needs a Chronicle kernel 19.84 or later; Arc now requires Cratis.Chronicle 19.90.0. (#3423)
- **Ante** ([Release v1.3.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Ante/releases/tag/v1.3.2)): Signed-in users keep their identity after the update to Arc 22.128: Ante opts in to trusting the identity headers AuthProxy forwards, which Arc no longer trusts by default. Ante must only be reachable through AuthProxy. (part of #174)
- **Ante** ([Release v1.3.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Ante/releases/tag/v1.3.2)): Requests other than GET and HEAD to a path the lobby does not serve, such as a CORS preflight, are refused again instead of receiving the application shell with a 200. (part of #174)
- **Ante** ([Release v1.3.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Ante/releases/tag/v1.3.2)): Projects that reference Ante, such as the integration specifications and the browser end-to-end host, build again in Debug: embedded Screenplay documents are turned off for every project, since Ante has no Screenplay model. (part of #174)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.128.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.128.2)): Embedding Screenplay documents with `Cratis.Arc.Screenplay.Embedded` no longer fails with a circular target dependency (MSB4006) in projects that reference Roslyn (directly or through `Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Analyzers`) and contain a `.resx` file. Such projects can turn embedding back on if they disabled it with `CratisEmbeddedScreenplayEnabled=false`. (#2979) (#3440)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.26.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.26.0)): A job stopped or removed while its silo went down is finished after recovery: steps that never reported are told to stop again, `OnStopped` runs once they have, and a job being removed is removed. See [Rehydration](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/blob/main/Documentation/concepts/jobs.md#rehydration). (#118)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.26.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.26.0)): A step's stop is counted once per execution, so a late or repeated stop report no longer makes the job's progress count it twice.
- **Scene** ([Release v4.72.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.72.0)): Lists and details of imported Screenplay screens showed only their column headings and labels in the Studio preview although the application's sample data pack held rows for the read model. (Cratis/StudioIssues#895)
- **Specifications** ([Release v4.1.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Specifications/releases/tag/v4.1.2)): CRSPEC0004 and the fact naming rule now recognize test attributes derived from xUnit and NUnit test attributes, such as a conditional `[DockerFact]` built on `[Fact]`, instead of reporting such specs as never running. (#52)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.227.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.227.1)): Proposing changes through the MCP server no longer slows down sharply as a model grows: fixture references in specifications are resolved against declarations computed once per workspace snapshot instead of rescanning every declaration for each reference. On a 56-file model `propose-source` drops from about 30 seconds of CPU to about 3, and a 260-file model that never finished now completes. (#938)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.177.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.177.2)): A projection with several `from` blocks, a key that is a payload property of events sourced by something else (a movement id on an event sourced by the SKU), or a constant mapping (`increased = true`) renders instead of being refused as STAGE-ESM-008 or STAGE-ESM-009. Screenplay keeps these as flat transitions; the renderer now reads them as the scope that says the same, so the scoped admission and renderer decide (part of #558)
- **release-action** ([Release v1.4.3](https://github.com/Cratis/release-action/releases/tag/v1.4.3)): When GitHub refuses to create the release with 403 because the release commit's `.github/workflows` differ from the default branch (another pull request changed workflows while this release was pending), the action now fails with an error that names that cause and the recovery: run the publish workflow with `workflow_dispatch` from the current default branch with the pull request's release notes instead of re-running. Other 403s keep their original error. (#215)
- **Components** ([Release v4.33.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Components/releases/tag/v4.33.0)): `Dialog` and `CommandDialog` keep keyboard focus inside the dialog while busy: the confirm button stays focusable as `aria-disabled` and ignores activation, so it can no longer drop focus to the page or be submitted twice. After a refused confirm, such as a validation failure, focus returns to the confirm button (or to the title when confirm is disabled), the outcome is announced through a polite status region, and a held Enter or Space no longer submits again. (#424)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.177.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.177.1)): A command whose productions address different event sources (`for orderId` and `for productId`, ESM v30) renders instead of being refused as STAGE-ESM-006: each event is appended to the event source its own destination names, and the generated command specification asserts each event on its own source (part of #503)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.177.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.177.0)): A zero start of a running total (`add delivered by 0`, `subtract x by 0` on a required whole number or decimal) now renders instead of being refused as STAGE-ESM-017; the renderer emits nothing for it because Chronicle's Add builder takes an event property only and a required number is 0 until its first real operation. A concept total does not start at 0 (a never-set concept is null) and is refused (part of #558)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.128.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.128.1)): The generated Screenplay no longer states a scenario fixture value that a concept's implicit conversion changes at runtime. A scalar constant, collection element or expected event value converted to a concept is stated only when the conversion operator and the constructor it calls are proven to pass the value through unchanged, including operators declared in another project of the application and the Cratis framework's own concept conversions; otherwise the whole scenario is left out with `SP0039`. Date comparisons keep their existing, stricter proof. See [Generating a Screenplay](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/blob/main/Documentation/backend/csharp/generating-a-screenplay.md). (#3385)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.128.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.128.0)): Protected command reads reject empty, whitespace-only, wildcard (`*`), hash-containing and whitespace-padded keys, including invalid composite key parts, before accessing the store. These failures use the same validation outcome as a missing read model rather than an unhandled server error. Composite key parts are checked through the AOT-safe protected-key accessors, so with `ProtectedReadModelKeyOptions.Strict` (or under native AOT) a composite key type without registered accessors fails with `ProtectedReadModelKeyAccessorMissing` instead of being read. (#3347)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.127.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.127.0)): An enumerable observable query over WebSocket now ends when the client disconnects while no item is ready, instead of keeping the connection handler waiting. (part of #2915)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.89.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.89.0)): .NET client calls that take a `CancellationToken` no longer block the calling thread while the client reconnects, and they honor the token while they wait for the connection. Previously such a call made while disconnected blocked synchronously for up to the connection timeout and ignored cancellation. (#4758)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.169.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.169.1)): The live semantic Host refuses protected command reads with empty, whitespace-only, padded, `*` or `#`-containing keys before looking up projected state, matching the specification executor and the Screenplay reference evaluator, even when an instance exists at that key. (#543)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.88.5](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.88.5)): A unit of work that enforces read protection no longer reports a conflict on historical events when its declared routing only matched events older than the read. Against a kernel without support for several guards per event source, the merged guard took the routing's filtered tail instead of the read boundary, so the commit was refused and its events were not stored. (#5070)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.125.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.125.4)): An observable query over MongoDB no longer stays on "Loading…" when the database is unreachable or the change stream fails. The MongoDB watch now ends the observable with an error, logged at Error level, instead of completing silently. Cancellation and normal unsubscribe still complete quietly. (#2916)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.125.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.125.4)): The SSE and WebSocket hub connections in `@cratis/arc` deliver a query error from the server as a failed query result (`hasExceptions` with the error message), so `useObservableQuery` shows the failure instead of only writing it to the console. (#2916)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.167.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.167.1)): Rendered applications no longer contain protected-read commands that are refused at run time (Acme Commerce Sales: `ApproveOrder`, `CheckOut`, `AddToBasket` and others failed 30 generated specifications).
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.88.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.88.4)): An observer no longer keeps partitions marked in flight forever, or undercounts its handled events, when events are handled while it changes state, for example during a large catch-up. Handled counts reset by a replay that starts during the change stay reset. (part of #4972)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.125.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.125.3)): Observable MongoDB queries keep updating after a Chronicle projection replay. When a collection is renamed into place, or the change stream reports `invalidate`, the shared watcher now sends fresh results to the observers of the affected collections. (#3407)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.125.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.125.3)): When MongoDB rejects an expired or invalid resume token, the shared watcher reopens the change stream without the token and resynchronizes every observer. Previously the stream kept retrying with the bad token and observers silently stopped updating. (#3407)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.125.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.125.3)): A failed snapshot for an observable MongoDB query is logged and retried a bounded number of times, and the subscription stays open instead of ending with an error. (#3407)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.167.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.167.0)): Acme Commerce Sales `AnApprovedOrderCannotBeApprovedAgain` (a rejection by the decision read of `ApproveOrder`, whose event triggers `ConfirmApprovedOrder`) was the last refusal of the Catalog + Storefront + Sales slice.
- **Arc** ([Release v22.125.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.125.2)): Data annotation attributes such as `[Range]`, `[Required]`, `[StringLength]` and custom `ValidationAttribute`s on ordinary parameters of model-bound queries are now evaluated, the same way they are on command properties. Previously only concept-typed parameters were validated, so for example `Top([Range(1, 100)] int count)` accepted any value. An omitted parameter with a declared default still uses its default. (#3378)
- **Components** ([Release v4.30.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Components/releases/tag/v4.30.0)): `DataTableForQuery`, `DataTableForObservableQuery` and `DataPage` show the failure message, not the empty message, when a query completes without success, for example on an HTTP 400 problem-details response. The query-bound chat sidebar likewise shows its failed state. An unauthorized result still shows access denied. (#406)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.88.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.88.3)): Projections no longer fail a partition with "Collection was modified; enumeration operation may not execute." when many events are handled in parallel. Values taken from a projection's initial state or from an event's content are now copied before they become part of one read model's state, so another partition can no longer change them while the MongoDB sink saves. (part of #4965)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.125.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.125.1)): Commands executed inside `ISystemExecution.As(principal)` without an HTTP request, including from Chronicle reactors, now record that authenticated principal as the event's `causedBy` instead of System. Events appended during an HTTP request and identities explicitly selected on Chronicle are recorded as before. (#3152)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.166.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.166.1)): Specifications whose `given` events have no event source now run in Stage like they do in the Screenplay reference evaluator, instead of being refused for identity allocation. The events land at an empty event source; projections that need a typed event source are still refused. (part of #511)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.88.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.88.1)): SQL observer state now preserves failed-partition quarantine and retry-budget resets when saved and reloaded. (#5039)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.166.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.166.0)): Acme Commerce Sales (`OrderStateProjection`, `ConfirmOrder`) can be planned for rendering without stripping: its projection and decision read of the order lines and shipping address were refused.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.88.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.88.0)): Registering a reactor, reducer or self-reducing read model whose only handlers take `[EventTypeGenerationFor]` types now fails with `ObserverHasOnlyGenerationHandlers`, naming the artifact and the generation types. Such an observer previously registered without any event types and never became active. (#4922)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.88.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.88.0)): A projection declaration that uses an `$eventContext` member or path that does not exist on the event context is now rejected when it is compiled. It was previously accepted and failed only when the projection was built. (#4702)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.164.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.164.0)): The semantic Host's JSON binder keeps omitted composite members omitted instead of turning them into `null`, and keeps the supplied member order, so nested concept validation fails only on an explicit `null` and reports failures in the order the members were sent. (#441)
- **AI** ([Release v7.84.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.84.2)): The local release-note check (`cratis-check-pr`) and the pull-request body guard recognize repositories that call the combined `verify-pull-request-policy` workflow, and take the release-note rules from that workflow at the caller's pinned ref. Repositories switched to the combined caller had silently stopped getting the local check. (#797)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.163.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.163.0)): A screen that fills a blueprint template slot now replaces that slot's seeded placeholder content instead of appearing next to it, so rendered applications no longer show the template's sample header, search box and empty states beside the authored content. Slots the screen does not fill keep their seeded content. (#506)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.221.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.221.0)): `SMELL005` no longer counts every known event as a feeder of a read-model property set in a projection `all` block. It counts only the events that actually feed it. (part of #393)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.87.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.87.0)): The .NET client now connects to the kernel Aspire references with `WithReference(chronicle)`. With no Chronicle connection string configured, it reads `ConnectionStrings:chronicle` (the value Aspire injects) instead of falling back to `localhost:35000`, so a referenced project no longer needs the kernel on host port 35000. A connection string set in `Cratis:Chronicle` or in code still takes precedence. (#4917)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.220.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.220.2)): Renaming a composite stream-id part whose name equals its type, or an event source or stream whose name also appears in a trailing comment, now succeeds under `PreserveTrivia` and keeps the rest of the line byte-for-byte, instead of refusing with "No unique supported identifier". (#572)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.86.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.86.0)): A reactor or reducer result now completes only the delivery it answers. The kernel matched results by partition alone, so a late result for a timed-out delivery could complete the wait of that partition's retry, and completing one delivery could drop the next one for the same partition and leave it blocked until the client reconnected. A duplicate result no longer breaks the result stream. (part of #4664)
- **AI** ([Release v7.84.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.84.1)): Publishing works again: the check that the GitHub release exists now runs after release-action has created the release, instead of before it, so a release-labelled merge publishes Cratis.AI, @cratis/ai, the Pi package and the images under its own GitHub release. (#785)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.160.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.160.0)): Acme Commerce commands `ApproveProductForSale`, `WithdrawProduct` (reads plus a unique constraint), `AddToBasket` and `CheckOut` (read-backed requirement plus validated concepts) were refused by the renderer although the executor ran them.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.85.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.85.1)): SQLite: a development reset no longer races with database migrations. Before, the reset could leave a database that failed every later request with `DatabaseMigrationIncomplete` ("table already exists"). Migrations and resets of the same database file are now serialized, and the reset drops its tables in one transaction. (#5007)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.85.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.85.1)): SQLite: concurrent first-time migrations of the same database through different connection strings no longer fail with `DatabaseMigrationIncomplete`. (#4894)
- **Scene** ([Release v4.70.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.70.2)): The Cratis Components command form's `Generate fields` action, its command property editor and its layout editor submitted `SetProperty` edits addressed by the bare element id, and the action's generated fields carried no item id. A host applies edits to the document by node id (`element:<id>`, `elementNodeId`) and the editing engine refuses a collection item without an id (`'Fields': every item needs an id`), so the editor refused the batch and nothing changed. The edits now address the element by its node id and each generated field has an id, so Generate fields applies and is one undo step in the host's history (part of Cratis/StudioIssues#863)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.158.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.158.1)): Specifications in a rendered application's separate Specs project now compile and run in Release as well as Debug. They are no longer excluded by `#if DEBUG`, while the web application continues to exclude the specification files. (part of #498)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.158.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.158.1)): Rendered specifications declare repeated and complex fixture values once as named fields and use separate facts for expected properties, instead of repeating literals in long predicates. (part of #498)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.158.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.158.1)): Rendered authorization-denial specifications assert that the command is denied and appends no events, rather than merely checking policy registration. (part of #498)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.83.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.83.4)): The in-memory sink used by the testing scenarios no longer merges distinct composite read-model keys whose parts contain `_`. Each distinct composite key keeps its own instance. (#5057)
- **Scene** ([Release v4.70.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.70.1)): A screen that fills a blueprint template slot now replaces that slot's seeded placeholder content instead of adding to it, so rendered applications no longer show the template's sample header, search box and empty states next to the authored content. Slots the screen does not fill keep their seeded content. See Cratis/Stage#506.
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.25.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.25.0)): A job whose silo died while it was preparing or starting its steps is recovered: it keeps the same step IDs, prepares only the steps that were not prepared yet, never runs `PrepareSteps` or the start hook twice, and never counts a step's outcome twice. A saved but unstarted job is no longer removed as dead during rehydration, and a job left in a starting state after a restart is no longer reported as already running (#40)
- **Components** ([Release v4.29.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Components/releases/tag/v4.29.1)): `DataTableForQuery` and `DataTableForObservableQuery` show loading when their query arguments change, instead of the previous arguments' rows or unauthorized, failed or loaded status, and show cached results again without a loading flash when returning to earlier arguments. Requires `@cratis/arc` and `@cratis/arc.react` 22.122 or later. (#354)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.83.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.83.3)): A projection definition change now takes effect on every silo. A silo could keep using the previous definition until it restarted, so events of a newly added event type were skipped during live handling, catch-up and even a full replay, while the observer still advanced past them. (#4935)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.83.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.83.3)): A silo no longer reads an outdated projection definition after another silo changed or deleted it. Definitions are now always read from shared storage rather than a per-silo cache. (#5021)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.83.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.83.3)): Replaying or catching up a projection that is not registered now fails instead of reporting success without handling any events. An event the current projection definition does not include is logged at information level rather than debug. (#4935)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.24.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.24.3)): Removing or stopping a job no longer hangs when a step's notification fails, for example because the step's grain is still routed to a silo that died. The job retries the notification with backoff (1 s doubling to 60 s) until the step reports, and a repeated `Remove` sends it again to every step that has not reported (#101)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.83.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.83.2)): Projection futures (child events stored until their parent exists) now resolve when the parent is matched on a composite or object key. Key comparison threw `InvalidCastException: Object must implement IConvertible` for such keys, so the future never resolved, the child event stayed missing from the read model, and the same warning was logged on every pass. A future that keeps failing now logs one warning and then logs at debug level. (#5045)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.122.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.122.1)): In decision mode, `CommandScenario` rebuilds read models from the scenario's appended events with their original `EventContext`. Projections and reducers see each event's real sequence number and occurred time, so specs that compare revisions no longer report false conflicts. (#3351)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.122.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.122.1)): Each decision-mode `CommandScenario` uses its own generated event store name and namespace, so scenarios no longer share one event store identity. (#2991)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.216.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.216.1)): The TypeScript compiler, Monaco and VS Code now report `PLAY0345`, `PLAY0346` and `PLAY0348` for guarded interaction alternatives (`on click`, `double click` and `select` with `when` branches), including named behaviors attached with `uses`, with the same messages, locations and order as the C# compiler. (#625)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.83.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.83.1)): An all-event mapping (`[FromEvery]`, `[FromAll]`, a declarative `FromEvery`, or `@fromAllEvents` in TypeScript) no longer clears its read model property when an event without the mapped payload property arrives. The property keeps its last value, as documented, for root mappings, child mappings and projections that subscribe to all events. A source property that is present with a null value still maps null. (#5026)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.154.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.154.1)): Semantic specification runs reject protected command reads with empty, whitespace-only, padded, `*` or `#`-containing keys before looking up projected state, even when an instance exists at that key. Live Host behavior is unchanged. (part of #492)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.214.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.214.3)): A capture specification that presents a numeric key to an event with a text event source still fails, but the failure now names the capture, the key, its value, the event and the correction, for example `write "1201"`, instead of only reporting `Expected Accepted, got Rejected`. The capture documentation states that the key must match the event source type. (#788)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.214.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.214.2)): Compilation now reports `PLAY0802` when an event stream's stored name is `All` or an event source's stored name is `Default`, including through an `id` pin, because Chronicle reserves those names. Before, the model compiled and only executable binding or rendering refused it. A pin to another stored name, such as `stream All` with `id "Archive"`, remains valid, and the editors show the error as you type. (#893)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.214.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.214.1)): `screenplay test` and the MCP run-specifications tool no longer report every specification as unsupported when one projection cannot be executed by the reference evaluator, for example one that maps `$eventContext.occurred`. Only specifications whose events reach that projection, or that supply, read, query or assert its read model, report unsupported; the others run. Plan issues are still listed. (#863)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.122.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.122.0)): The Chronicle sample's Approvals specifications state their approval identity and the whole `ApprovalRequested` event, so both scenarios round-trip into the generated Screenplay instead of being left out with SP0039. (#3383)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.152.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.152.0)): Commands sent from a rendered application lost the API base path, the microservice and the identity headers that Arc adds, so they could reach the wrong route or arrive unauthenticated. (part of #499)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.121.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.121.2)): Data annotations on concept-typed (`ConceptAs<T>`) command properties and model-bound query parameters are now evaluated against the concept's value: `[Required]` rejects a concept wrapping an empty string, `[Range]` accepts valid values, and `[StringLength]` no longer throws. The validation metadata is source-generated, so it stays trim- and AOT-safe. Types without generated metadata, such as types from other assemblies, keep the previous behavior. (#2944)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.24.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.24.2)): Automatic job recovery now retries a jobs manager key provider that fails to construct during startup, instead of skipping its scopes until another recovery pass. Construction failures use the existing recovery backoff and stop when the silo shuts down. (#108)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.24.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.24.1)): Jobs with some steps that fail to start now enter the running state and keep their watchdog active until the remaining steps finish. They no longer remain stuck starting when a completion report is lost. `FailedStartingSomeJobSteps` still reports the partial failure; the other steps continue running. (#104)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.121.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.121.1)): Controller-based observable queries whose arguments fail validation over a direct WebSocket or SSE connection now accept the connection, send one query result with the validation results and close, instead of answering HTTP 400 before the upgrade, which browsers report only as a failed connection. Plain HTTP requests keep their existing response. (#3230)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.213.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.213.4)): Events produced only by a reaction's refusal branch (`on refused ...`) now count as producers: the destination type of an event source without an identifier is inferred from them, and a specification's expected route or `for` type is no longer refused as contradicting the command under test when a refusal branch also produces the event. (#744)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.24.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.24.0)): Removing a job no longer reports success when the removal cannot be saved. Before, a storage error left the job running in storage, and a silo restart could resume a job you had deleted. Remove now refuses with the new `RemoveJobError.CouldNotPersistRemoval`, and `IJobs.Delete` returns it as a refusal you can retry. (#105)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.213.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.213.3)): Compilation now reports `PLAY0168` at the second declaration when one document declares the same policy name twice. Before, the duplicate compiled cleanly and surfaced only at executable binding as an unlocated `PLAY0273`. (#787)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.213.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.213.3)): The TypeScript compiler, and so the editors, now report `PLAY0173` when one policy name is declared in two files, as the C# compiler already did. (#787)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.23.5](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.23.5)): A finished job no longer stays running in storage when clearing or writing its final state fails during a storage outage. The job now writes its final status before clearing its state, and keeps retrying that write (from 1 second up to 60 seconds) until it succeeds. If the clear fails, the job is still recorded as finished and retention cleanup removes the leftover record. A finished job is not completed a second time after a restart. (#100)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.120.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.120.1)): PostgreSQL migrations that add a JSON column with `AddJsonColumn` now run. The CHECK constraint and the GIN index are emitted as separate, terminated statements instead of one invalid statement (`42601`), the index name is quoted, and the index works whatever store type the column uses by indexing `column::jsonb`. Constraint and index names longer than PostgreSQL's 63-byte limit are shortened deterministically with a stable hash suffix. (#3369)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.213.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.213.2)): `ComparedModel.FromSources` accepts documents in subfolders, such as `Features/Register.play`, so split models can be compared from in-memory sources. Each document keeps its relative path. (#628)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.213.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.213.1)): Executable binding (`screenplay test`, `cratis screenplay validate --executable`) now reports every independent semantic contract failure (`PLAY0273`) in one run, each at the declaration that failed, instead of only the first failure at line 1 of an unrelated file. (#860)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.213.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.213.1)): A condition that compares an optional property, such as `require level.status == "active"` on an optional read-model property, is now refused with `PLAY0268` at the comparison instead of a `PLAY0273` without a location. (#860)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.23.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.23.4)): Resuming a job no longer stops at the first step whose start fails with an error, for example because its silo is unavailable or the call times out after a pod crash. The remaining steps still start, and the job's watchdog starts the failed step again on its next check, without step leases or another recovery pass. (#102)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.82.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.82.2)): Unique constraints and closing constraints stay consistent with the event log when updating their index fails after an append, or when the kernel stops in between. Each event sequence persists how far its constraint indexes are confirmed in MongoDB, SQL and in-memory storage. Before validating an append, it repairs any gap from the log, and it catches older indexes up in the background without blocking live appends. (#4895)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.82.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.82.2)): Constraint index repairs write only the claims that actually differ, report a budget expiry or a failing constraint in their results, and recover from transient storage failures instead of leaving the index partly updated. (#4895)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.23.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.23.3)): Job recovery now retries a jobs manager key provider or a manager rehydrate that failed, for example a key catalog that times out while a rolling deployment starts. It retries only the failed providers and managers, after 1 second, doubling the delay up to 60 seconds, until they succeed or the silo stops. Jobs left running by a silo that went away are no longer stuck until another silo dies. (#99)
- **AI** ([Release v7.75.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.75.0)): `failFast: false` in a gate configuration is honored. Previously it was read as `true`. (#753)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.212.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.212.4)): `automap` and `no automap` written on a joined event now decide auto-mapping for that event in the executable model and the reference evaluator, as they do in Chronicle; a joined event without one follows its level's auto-map. Warning `PLAY0380` no longer reports them and now covers only `all` inside `children` or `nested`. (#613)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.117.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.117.1)): MongoDB observations no longer drop updates, replacements and deletions of documents with a composite `_id`: the change-stream id is now read with the id member's own serializer, so it compares with the observed documents for any id type. (#3236)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.117.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.117.1)): Observing a collection inside a single-result query no longer leaks the collection's paging and total count into the query result. `ObserveSingle` and `ObserveById` never apply query paging or counting, and observable query results report paging only for collection results. The `ignoreQueryContext` parameter on `ObserveSingle` and `ObserveById` is kept and has no effect. (#3023)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.82.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.82.1)): SQL storage now returns the revised content, generation and hash when it reads an event that was revised at a generation lower than the highest generation stored for it, matching MongoDB and in-memory storage. Before, reactors, reducers, webhooks, event-store subscriptions, passive projections, event reads, export and constraint reindexing could see the pre-revision content. Events revised before Chronicle recorded which generation was revised keep returning their highest generation. (#4751)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.82.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.82.1)): Redacting an event in SQL storage no longer leaves it marked as revised. (#4751)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.212.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.212.3)): Compilation now reports `PLAY0801` when a projection uses one event more than once at the same level. Before, the error only appeared at executable binding, as the generic `PLAY0273`. A level is the root, one `children` or `nested` block, or one variant. Within a level, `from`, `remove with`, `remove via join` and `clear with` share one set of events, and `join` events have their own. Chronicle allows one use of an event type per projection level. (#675)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.212.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.212.3)): The task-tracking counter example in the projections documentation no longer uses the same event in two `from` blocks. (#675)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.31.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.31.2)): An identity too large for a cookie (over 3072 encoded characters) is no longer written to `.cratis-identity`: browsers drop such a cookie and a reverse proxy answers 502 "upstream sent too big header" for the whole response. Any stale identity cookie is expired and a warning is logged instead.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.81.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.81.1)): Projections and captures are subscribed in every namespace, including one created at runtime. Ensuring a namespace returns once its healthy projections and captures are subscribed, and a namespace created implicitly by its first append is subscribed within one watchdog interval, without depending on the namespace-added notification. (#4696) (#4455)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.81.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.81.1)): A projection that lost its subscription in a namespace, for example after a retirement or replay in the same registration, is subscribed again instead of staying dark until the next change. (#4423)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.81.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.81.1)): Captures follow the same reliable namespace reconciliation, so a pattern or events capture is subscribed in namespaces an application starts using after registration. (#4521)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.81.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.81.1)): Automatic reconciliation never lifts an observer's quarantine, never revives a retired observer, and never subscribes a capture that is being stopped or deleted; a failed setup is retried, and one broken projection or capture no longer blocks the others or namespace creation. A Kernel restart and a newly added namespace still end projection quarantine as documented.
- **Scene** ([Release v4.69.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.69.3)): The gallery chrome drew a fixed Contoso logo and name, so a host that gives the preview its own `chrome` could not show the profile's identity. `galleryCompositionFor(identity)` draws the resolved identity - its name, initials, icon or image and whether the name shows - in the topbar, the sidebar and the footer, and `galleryComposition` is unchanged for a host that has no identity
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.212.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.212.2)): `find-specification-obligations` now counts the documented read-only denial form, `then query <Query>` with `arguments` and no result followed by `then denied`, as the authorization denial specification for that query (`SPEC004`), the same as `when query <Query>` with `then denied`. Such a specification no longer counts as asserting the read model (`SPEC006`). (#786)
- **Scene** ([Release v4.69.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.69.2)): `PreviewRegionHandle`, `PreviewRegionRect` and the region helpers were left out of the default blueprint's exports, so a host that passes `regions` to `ScenePreviewHost` could not type the ref. They are exported from `@cratis/scene.blueprint.default` now
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.23.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.23.2)): A job step that is stopped without a partial result no longer reports an empty string as its partial result: `HasPartialResult` is false and `TryGetPartialResult` returns false for it. (#93)
- **cli** ([Release v3.77.1](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.77.1)): `cratis ai install` and `cratis ai update` link `.pi/agents` to the corpus's Pi-native agent adapters (`.cratis/ai/harnesses/pi/agents`) when the corpus ships them, so Pi agent loaders other than Cratis' own, such as `@tintinweb/pi-subagents`, give Cratis agents their read, search, edit and shell tools. An unchanged Cratis-owned link is moved on update; corpora without the adapters keep the canonical `.cratis/ai/agents` link. (#391)
- **Scene** ([Release v4.69.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.69.1)): Adding the identity, corners, font, sample data pack description, template shell card text and problem severity to positional records removed the constructors an earlier package was compiled against, so `UiProfile`, `ProfileAppearance`, `SampleDataPackReference`, `TemplateMetadata`, `SampleDataCatalogEntry` and `ProfileBlocker` threw `MissingMethodException` for callers built before. Each keeps the constructor it had, the new one is marked for the JSON serializer, and a specification names every shape that must stay
- **Arc** ([Release v22.116.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.116.2)): Aggregate roots resolve event types from the registered event types instead of the `[EventType]` attribute, so events registered explicitly without the attribute can be applied, rehydrated and appended. Applying an event that is not registered still fails. (#3234)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.116.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.116.2)): A command started from an execution context captured during an earlier, finished command is no longer refused because the finished command's identity still looked active. (#2888)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.80.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.80.0)): Catch-up that produces no work no longer loops between Routing, CatchUp and CaughtUp without backoff. (#4592)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.212.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.212.1)): `--check query-keys` (PLAY0535) no longer warns when a collection query's `by` parameter names a declared read-model field of a compatible type, or when a `by` parameter is marked `optional`. A single-result query's `by` must still match the read model's identity, and the identity is now inferred only from single-result queries, as executable binding does. (#676)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.116.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.116.1)): `useObservableQuery` subscribes again with the new page, page size or sort order when they change, instead of staying on the first page. Hooks observing different pages of the same query no longer share one subscription. (#2869)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.116.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.116.1)): `useQuery`, `useQueryWithPaging` and `useObservableQuery` no longer show the previous arguments' data or status while results for new arguments, pages or sort order load: they show the cached result for the new arguments, or the initial not-ready state while the request is in flight. (#2872)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.23.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.23.1)): Stopping or removing a job after a silo restart, before its steps have started again, now finishes: each step reports its stop to the job, so the job counts it, finishes its stop and, for removal, clears its state. Previously the step reported to no one and the job never completed its stop or removal. (#92)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.23.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.23.1)): A step that cannot find its job when it is stopped no longer pretends the stop was reported: a direct stop returns `JobStepError.FailedToReportToJob`, the reason is logged, and the step keeps its state. Removing a step whose job no longer exists deletes it. See [Jobs](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/blob/main/Documentation/concepts/jobs.md). (part of #92)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.212.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.212.0)): Model-note targets that name a Module, Feature or Slice by dotted address resolve on split models, where the container is repeated across files as an import placement or an explicit header, instead of failing with "Unknown or ambiguous note address." (#784)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.212.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.212.0)): When a note's requested anchor has no identity yet, for example a Slice on a model that does not bind, the proposal reports in `substitutions` the owner the note actually lands on, also for reanchor, instead of silently attaching it to the application. (#795)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.23.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.23.0)): `Job.Stop` no longer reports a stop that was not persisted. It restores the job's state and answers the new `StopJobError.CouldNotPersistStop`.
- **AI** ([Release v7.73.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.73.1)): Corpus agents such as Screenplay Modeler keep their file and shell tools in Pi when the external `Agent` tool from `@tintinweb/pi-subagents` launches them. `.pi/agents` now points at generated Pi-native copies of the corpus agents with Pi tool names, and the Pi package ships them for that loader. Generating them fails when an agent names a tool that has no Pi equivalent. (#519)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.115.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.115.0)): Rejections from data annotations, and `ValidationResult`s returned from a command's `Provide()` or `Handle()` with `[nameof(Property)]` members, now show under the matching form field instead of disappearing. (#2945)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.141.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.141.1)): Specification runs now resolve `$context.occurred` in event-triggered reactions from the scenario's authored `given clock`, the same way Screenplay's reference execution does. The value reaches direct and cascading productions, invocation arguments, the invoked command and refusal-branch productions. Specifications that combine an authored clock with reactions are no longer refused, and a reaction that maps `occurred` without a clock is rejected as in the reference execution. (#505)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.79.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.79.0)): During a rolling deploy, an older client that registers its projections after a newer one no longer overwrites the newer definitions. The kernel compares versions by semantic-versioning precedence, prerelease identifiers included. It ignores a definition older than the one it has stored and logs a warning. It also raises a Workbench recommendation naming the projection and both versions. An older full-set registration no longer retires projections that a newer version added, and the kernel no longer writes a stale definition back while it resubscribes projections. To roll back on purpose, give the older release a `SoftwareVersion` higher than the stored one. Clients that send no version register as before. (#5012)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.22.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.22.3)): A state machine no longer drops a transition when a second one is requested while another transition is still in progress. Requests made during a transition now run in the order they were made, once the current transition completes. A queued transition is checked again against the state it would leave if another state was entered first, and is skipped if that state does not allow it. A transition the current state refuses still does nothing and is not an error; call `CanTransitionTo` first to branch on it. See [State machines](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/blob/main/Documentation/concepts/state-machines.md). (#58)
- **Chronicle.TypeScript** ([Release v6.53.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.TypeScript/releases/tag/v6.53.1)): A child or nested read-model property decorated with `@fromAllEvents` (or `@fromEvery`) is populated again, including on the event that creates the child. The compiler now declares these mappings on the child itself instead of on the root, so root properties are never copied into children and a property with the same name on the root and on a child is mapped independently. Requires Chronicle 19.78.1 or later for root-only all-event mappings to stay off new children; replay affected projections to rebuild child values that were already materialized. (#257)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.22.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.22.2)): A failed resume hook now restores a fully accounted-for stopped job to Stopped before any new steps start, so a later resume can retry instead of adopting a job left Running with no new work. Existing running jobs and incomplete stops are not rolled back. (#62)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.211.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.211.1)): Incompatible property mappings in command productions, reaction productions and reaction invocations now report PLAY0273 at the offending mapping, naming the property and its source and target types, instead of pointing to the first line of an unrelated file. (#785)
- **AI** ([Release v7.72.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.72.1)): Completions on Claude models of generation 5 and later (such as `claude-sonnet-5-5`) no longer fail with a 400 saying `thinking.type.enabled` is not supported. Those models are now asked for adaptive thinking with an effort level instead of a thinking budget.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.211.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.211.0)): A refused authoring proposal now names the offending document and path in the conflict and lists every offending reference occurrence (text, member, document path and line:column, capped at ten), instead of returning a conflict with no location. (#783)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.78.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.78.1)): A child read-model property marked `[FromAll]` (or `[FromEvery]`) under `[ChildrenFrom<T>]` or `[Nested]` is populated again, including on the event that creates the child. The .NET client now declares these mappings on the child itself, and parent all-event mappings are never copied into children. (#4934) (#4524)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.78.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.78.1)): Projection definitions registered by older .NET clients (and the current TypeScript client), which declare child all-event mappings on the root, populate the child as long as the root read model does not declare a property with the same name. When both the root and the child declare it, the mapping stays on the root until the client is upgraded. Replay affected projections to rebuild child values that were already materialized. See [From all events](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/blob/main/Documentation/projections/model-bound/from-all.mdx#child-and-nested-properties).
- **AI** ([Release v7.72.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.72.0)): A stored tier mapping whose model is absent from the provider's last successfully discovered catalog is refused with a reason instead of calling that model. Pools try the next member without recording a rate limit. Before upgrading, inspect production providers with `ResolveTiersFor` and change or clear refused mappings, including mappings saved automatically during initial discovery. (#345)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.113.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.113.1)): `useQuery`: a component whose request was cancelled by another component reading the same query no longer keeps its query scope performing after it unmounts or starts a newer request, and no longer updates its state after it has unmounted. The other component's request still completes and shows its data. (#3349)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.77.6](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.77.6)): In a multi-silo kernel, a projection definition saved through one silo no longer looks missing on the others. A changed projection now evolves and replays as it should, instead of keeping its old definition. Projection grains now start with their stored definition, and removing a projection's observer now also removes the projection. (#4985)
- **AI** ([Release v7.70.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.70.1)): The pull-request body guard no longer blocks `gh pr create` or `gh pr edit` when it cannot read a Cratis repository's release workflows, for example because the ambient GitHub credentials are invalid. It warns and checks the body against the bundled reviewed release-note rules instead, so a valid body goes through and an invalid one is still rejected. (#509)
- **Scene** ([Release v4.68.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.68.2)): The darkened tag severity colours were written with `light-dark()`. The Stage's CSS pipeline lowers `light-dark()` for older browsers into a pair of custom properties that stays unset there, so a severity tag had **no background** (white text on the page, 1.09:1) in the Stage. The shell now sets the light values and overrides them under the Scene configuration's `data-scene-color-scheme='dark'` (Lara's own dark values), with no `light-dark()` in the stylesheet (a specification keeps it that way).
- **Scene** ([Release v4.68.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.68.2)): The real-browser template audit did not draw the dark page: its host kept the colour scheme of its first render, so the "dark theme" rows re-judged the light page. The host now remounts per scheme (and a specification alternates light and dark to prove it); the whole dark matrix - every template, state and width - passes.
- **Ante** ([Release v1.3.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Ante/releases/tag/v1.3.1)): Registration and organization setup no longer delete the identity cookie, which nothing writes any more, so Ante builds against current Arc.
- **Ante** ([Release v1.3.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Ante/releases/tag/v1.3.1)): A collection expression is indented as the style analyzer expects.
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.22.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.22.1)): The error enums nested in `IJobTypes` document their items, so the package update builds again.
- **AI** ([Release v7.70.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.70.0)): The provider capacity documentation no longer claims every path parks a provider that ran out of quota: `ProviderAwareLanguageModel` and `PooledChatClient` park it, while the standalone `IAIProviderPoolDispatcher` only fails over. (#569)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.113.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.113.0)): Under read protection `On`, a read model injected outside the command's decision, such as into a custom command filter, is refused with a message that names the fix; a read model marked `[Unprotected("why")]` there is an advisory read (#3242)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.77.5](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.77.5)): A new event store is now announced reliably when a client connects while the kernel is still starting. Before, the announcement could be refused because the System event types weren't registered yet. The client's registration then failed and was retried in the background, which could re-apply an older projection definition over a newer one. (#4998)
- **Scene** ([Release v4.68.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.68.1)): The bare `core:button` was 21 pixels high (WCAG 2.2 target size): the shell now gives a button in the content area, in the application and the full-page shell, a 2rem minimum.
- **Scene** ([Release v4.68.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.68.1)): Two `search` landmarks on one page (topbar and list) had no accessible names: `foundation:globalSearch` now names its form with its label.
- **Scene** ([Release v4.68.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.68.1)): A grid item kept `min-width: auto`, so a widget holding a wide table pushed its track past the window: a phone scrolled sideways and, on a desktop, the dashboard's primary column overlapped its secondary one. Every item of a template slot now shrinks to its track and the table scrolls inside itself.
- **Scene** ([Release v4.68.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v4.68.1)): PrimeReact's Lara tag severities (info, success, warn, danger) paint white on a mid-tone in the light scheme (2.3 to 3.8:1): the blueprint darkens them to meet WCAG AA, the dark scheme's values are unchanged.
